import cv2
import json
import csv
import argparse
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part1_coordinates.json")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

FILL_THRESHOLD = 55
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 28
BUBBLE_RADIUS = 5

CHOICES = ["A", "B", "C", "D"]


# ==========================================
# DOC GIA TRI TAM O TRON
# ==========================================

def get_center_value(gray, x, y):

    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))

    r = BUBBLE_RADIUS

    roi = gray[
        y-r:y+r+1,
        x-r:x+r+1
    ]

    if roi.shape != (2*r+1, 2*r+1):
        raise ValueError("O dap an nam ngoai anh.")

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r:r+1, -r:r+1]

    mask = xx**2 + yy**2 <= r**2

    return float(np.median(roi[mask]))


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN O TO
# ==========================================

def detect_part1(image_path):

    # Doc anh bai lam da can chinh
    student = cv2.imread(str(image_path))

    if student is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh: {image_path}"
        )

    # Doc anh phieu mau trang
    template = cv2.imread(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))

    if template is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "Khong tim thay anh phieu mau."
        )

    # Doc toa do 160 o
    with open(
        COORDINATE_PATH,
        "r",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:
        coordinate_data = json.load(f)

    if student.shape[:2] != template.shape[:2]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Kich thuoc anh bai lam khong khop phieu mau. "
            "Hay chay align_sheet.py truoc."
        )

    # Chuyen sang anh xam
    student_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        student,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    template_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        template,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    questions = coordinate_data["questions"]

    if len(questions) != 40:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Can 40 cau, hien co {len(questions)} cau."
        )

    results = []
    debug = student.copy()

    print("\nKET QUA NHAN DIEN PHAN I")
    print("=" * 65)

    for question in questions:

        q_number = question["question"]

        scores = {}

        # Doc do den cua 4 phuong an
        for choice in CHOICES:

            x, y = question["choices"][choice]

            reference_value = get_center_value(
                template_gray, x, y
            )

            student_value = get_center_value(
                student_gray, x, y
            )

            # Do muc do toi hon so voi phieu mau
            score = reference_value - student_value

            scores[choice] = round(
                max(0, score), 1
            )

        # Tim cac phuong an duoc to ro
        
        # ======================================
        # PHAN LOAI DAP AN THEO TUONG QUAN
        # ======================================

        ranked = sorted(
            scores.items(),
            key=lambda item: item[1],
            reverse=True
        )

        top_choice, top_score = ranked[0]
        second_choice, second_score = ranked[1]

        # Lay 2 diem thap nhat lam muc nen
        lowest_two = sorted(scores.values())[:2]
        baseline = float(np.mean(lowest_two))

        top_margin = top_score - baseline
        second_margin = second_score - baseline

        MARK_MARGIN = 55
        REVIEW_MARGIN = 30

        filled = []
        uncertain = []

        if top_margin >= MARK_MARGIN:

            if second_margin >= MARK_MARGIN:
                answer = "MULTIPLE"
                status = "MULTIPLE_MARKS"

                filled = [
                    choice
                    for choice in CHOICES
                    if scores[choice] - baseline >= MARK_MARGIN
                ]

            else:
                answer = top_choice
                status = "VALID"
                filled = [top_choice]

        elif top_margin >= REVIEW_MARGIN:

            answer = "REVIEW"
            status = "UNCERTAIN"
            uncertain = [top_choice]

        else:

            answer = ""
            status = "BLANK"

        
        # ======================================
        # LUU KET QUA CUA TUNG CAU
        # ======================================

        results.append({
            "question": q_number,
            "answer": answer,
            "status": status,
            "scores": scores.copy()
        })

        print(
            f"Cau {q_number:02d}: "
            f"{answer if answer else 'BO TRONG'} "
            f"| {status}"
        )

        # Danh dau cac o tren anh kiem tra
        for choice in CHOICES:

            x, y = question["choices"][choice]
            x, y = int(x), int(y)

            if choice in filled:
                color = (0, 0, 255)       # Do: da to

            elif choice in uncertain:
                color = (0, 165, 255)     # Cam: can kiem tra

            else:
                color = (150, 150, 150)   # Xam: bo trong

            cv2.circle(
                debug,
                (x, y),
                9,
                color,
                2
            )

        # Ghi dap an nhan dang
        first_x, first_y = question["choices"]["D"]

        # Ghi dap an nhan dang
        first_x, first_y = question["choices"]["D"]

        cv2.putText(
            debug,
            answer if answer else "-",
            (int(first_x) + 12, int(first_y) + 4),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.4,
            (255, 0, 0),
            1
        )

    # ======================================
    # LUU KET QUA CSV VA JSON
    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem

    csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part1.csv"
    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part1.json"
    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part1_debug.png"

    with open(
        csv_path,
        "w",
        newline="",
        encoding="utf-8-sig"
    ) as f:

        writer = csv.writer(f)

        writer.writerow([
            "Cau",
            "Dap an",
            "Trang thai",
            "Diem A",
            "Diem B",
            "Diem C",
            "Diem D"
        ])

        for item in results:

            writer.writerow([
                item["question"],
                item["answer"],
                item["status"],
                item["scores"]["A"],
                item["scores"]["B"],
                item["scores"]["C"],
                item["scores"]["D"]
            ])

    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            results,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    cv2.imwrite(
        str(debug_path),
        debug
    )

    # ======================================
    # THONG KE
    # ======================================

    valid = sum(
        r["status"] == "VALID"
        for r in results
    )

    blank = sum(
        r["status"] == "BLANK"
        for r in results
    )

    multiple = sum(
        r["status"] == "MULTIPLE_MARKS"
        for r in results
    )

    uncertain = sum(
        r["status"] == "UNCERTAIN"
        for r in results
    )

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print("THONG KE NHAN DIEN")
    print("Nhan dang mot dap an:", valid)
    print("Bo trong:", blank)
    print("To nhieu dap an:", multiple)
    print("Can kiem tra:", uncertain)

    print("\nDa luu:")
    print(csv_path.resolve())
    print(json_path.resolve())
    print(debug_path.resolve())


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--image",
        default="output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png",
        help="Anh bai lam da can chinh"
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:

        detect_part1(Path(args.image))

    except Exception as error:

        print("\nLOI:", error)

import cv2
import json
import csv
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part2_coordinates.json")

DEFAULT_STUDENT_IMAGE = Path(
    "output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png"
)

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

CHOICES = ["true", "false"]
ROWS = ["a", "b", "c", "d"]

# Nguong thu nghiem tren phieu photocopy
FILL_THRESHOLD = 70
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 58

BUBBLE_RADIUS = 5


# ==========================================
# DOC DO TOI TAI TAM O TRON
# ==========================================

def get_center_value(gray, x, y):

    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))

    radius = BUBBLE_RADIUS

    x1 = x - radius
    y1 = y - radius
    x2 = x + radius + 1
    y2 = y + radius + 1

    if (
        x1 < 0 or y1 < 0
        or x2 > gray.shape[1]
        or y2 > gray.shape[0]
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Toa do nam ngoai anh: ({x}, {y})"
        )

    roi = gray[y1:y2, x1:x2]

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[
        -radius:radius + 1,
        -radius:radius + 1
    ]

    mask = xx**2 + yy**2 <= radius**2

    return float(np.median(roi[mask]))


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN PHAN II
# ==========================================

def detect_part2(image_path):

    # Doc anh bai lam da can chinh
    student = cv2.imread(str(image_path))

    if student is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh bai lam: {image_path}"
        )

    # Doc anh phieu mau trang
    template = cv2.imread(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))

    if template is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "Khong tim thay anh phieu mau."
        )

    # Kiem tra kich thuoc
    if student.shape[:2] != template.shape[:2]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Kich thuoc anh khong khop phieu mau. "
            "Hay can chinh lai truoc."
        )

    # Doc file toa do
    with open(
        COORDINATE_PATH,
        "r",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:
        coordinate_data = json.load(f)

    questions = coordinate_data.get("questions", [])

    if len(questions) != 8:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Can 8 cau, hien co {len(questions)} cau."
        )

    # Chuyen sang anh xam
    student_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        student,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    template_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        template,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    debug = student.copy()

    results = []

    print("\nKET QUA NHAN DIEN PHAN II")
    print("=" * 65)

    # ======================================
    # DOC 8 CAU X 4 Y
    # ======================================

    for question in questions:

        q_number = question["question"]

        for row_label in ROWS:

            row_data = question["rows"][row_label]

            scores = {}

            # Doc hai o DUNG va SAI
            for choice in CHOICES:

                x, y = row_data[choice]

                reference_value = get_center_value(
                    template_gray, x, y
                )

                student_value = get_center_value(
                    student_gray, x, y
                )

                # So sanh voi phieu mau
                score = reference_value - student_value

                scores[choice] = round(
                    max(0, score), 1
                )

            true_score = scores["true"]
            false_score = scores["false"]


            
            # ==================================
            # PHAN LOAI DUNG / SAI
            # ==================================

            true_score = scores["true"]
            false_score = scores["false"]

            if (
                true_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                and false_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
            ):
                answer = "MULTIPLE"
                status = "MULTIPLE_MARKS"

            elif (
                true_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                and false_score < REVIEW_THRESHOLD
            ):
                answer = "DUNG"
                status = "VALID"

            elif (
                false_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                and true_score < REVIEW_THRESHOLD
            ):
                answer = "SAI"
                status = "VALID"

            elif max(true_score, false_score) >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
                answer = "REVIEW"
                status = "UNCERTAIN"

            else:
                answer = ""
                status = "BLANK"


            # ==================================
            # LUU KET QUA
            # ==================================

            results.append({
                "question": q_number,
                "statement": row_label,
                "answer": answer,
                "status": status,
                "true_score": true_score,
                "false_score": false_score
            })

            print(
                f"Cau {q_number:02d} - {row_label}: "
                f"{answer if answer else 'BO TRONG':10s} "
                f"| Dung={true_score:5.1f} "
                f"Sai={false_score:5.1f}"
            )

            # ==================================
            # VE KET QUA LEN ANH
            # ==================================

            for choice in CHOICES:

                x, y = row_data[choice]

                x = int(round(x))
                y = int(round(y))

                score = scores[choice]

                if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
                    color = (0, 0, 255)

                elif score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
                    color = (0, 165, 255)

                else:
                    color = (150, 150, 150)

                cv2.circle(
                    debug,
                    (x, y),
                    9,
                    color,
                    2
                )

            # Ghi ket qua ben phai o Sai
            false_x, false_y = row_data["false"]

            display_answer = (
                answer if answer else "-"
            )

            cv2.putText(
                debug,
                display_answer,
                (int(false_x) + 12, int(false_y) + 4),
                cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                0.35,
                (255, 0, 0),
                1
            )

    # ======================================
    # THONG KE
    # ======================================

    valid = sum(
        r["status"] == "VALID"
        for r in results
    )

    blank = sum(
        r["status"] == "BLANK"
        for r in results
    )

    multiple = sum(
        r["status"] == "MULTIPLE_MARKS"
        for r in results
    )

    uncertain = sum(
        r["status"] == "UNCERTAIN"
        for r in results
    )

    # ======================================
    # LUU FILE KET QUA
    # Tao ten rieng de tranh ghi de CSV dang mo
    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem

    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S"
    )

    csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part2_{timestamp}.csv"
    )

    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part2_{timestamp}.json"
    )

    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part2_debug_{timestamp}.png"
    )

    # Luu CSV
    with open(
        csv_path,
        "w",
        newline="",
        encoding="utf-8-sig"
    ) as f:

        writer = csv.writer(f)

        writer.writerow([
            "Cau",
            "Y",
            "Dap an",
            "Trang thai",
            "Diem Dung",
            "Diem Sai"
        ])

        for item in results:

            writer.writerow([
                item["question"],
                item["statement"],
                item["answer"],
                item["status"],
                item["true_score"],
                item["false_score"]
            ])

    # Luu JSON
    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            results,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    # Luu anh kiem tra
    cv2.imwrite(
        str(debug_path),
        debug
    )

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print("THONG KE NHAN DIEN PHAN II")

    print("Nhan dang mot lua chon:", valid)
    print("Bo trong:", blank)
    print("To ca hai:", multiple)
    print("Can kiem tra:", uncertain)

    print("\nDA LUU KET QUA:")
    print(csv_path.resolve())
    print(json_path.resolve())
    print(debug_path.resolve())


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    import argparse

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--image",
        default=str(DEFAULT_STUDENT_IMAGE),
        help="Anh bai lam da can chinh"
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:

        detect_part2(Path(args.image))

    except Exception as error:

        print("\nLOI:", error)

[20/38] TẬP TIN: archive_backup/detect_part3.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import cv2
import json
import csv
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part3_coordinates.json")

DEFAULT_IMAGE = Path(
    "output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png"
)

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

# Nguong thu nghiem cho diem tuong phan cuc bo
# Diem moi khac thang diem cu (khong so sanh truc tiep 90/65 cu).
FILL_THRESHOLD = 32
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 15

# Chenh lech toi thieu giua chu so cao nhat va chu so thu hai
DIGIT_MARGIN = 12

# Ban kinh vung do:
# - vung loi nho ben trong tam o tron
# - vung nen vong ngoai, tranh duong vien in cua o tron
CORE_RADIUS = 3
RING_INNER_RADIUS = 9
RING_OUTER_RADIUS = 11


# ==========================================
# DO TUONG PHAN CUC BO - GIAM ANH HUONG BONG
# ==========================================

def get_local_contrast(gray, x, y):
    """
    Do su khac biet giua loi o tron va nen xung quanh.

    Bóng đổ diện rộng thường làm tối cả lõi và nền xung quanh.
    Lấy hiệu nền - lõi giúp giảm ảnh hưởng của thay đổi ánh sáng
    đồng đều trong vùng nhỏ.

    Vùng lõi tránh đường viền vòng tròn.
    Vùng nền là vành khăn bên ngoài đường viền.
    """

    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))

    outer = RING_OUTER_RADIUS

    x1 = x - outer
    y1 = y - outer
    x2 = x + outer + 1
    y2 = y + outer + 1

    if (
        x1 < 0 or y1 < 0
        or x2 > gray.shape[1]
        or y2 > gray.shape[0]
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Toa do ngoai anh: ({x}, {y})"
        )

    roi = gray[y1:y2, x1:x2].astype(np.float32)

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-outer:outer + 1, -outer:outer + 1]
    distance_sq = xx**2 + yy**2

    core_mask = distance_sq <= CORE_RADIUS**2

    ring_mask = (
        (distance_sq >= RING_INNER_RADIUS**2)
        & (distance_sq <= RING_OUTER_RADIUS**2)
    )

    core_pixels = roi[core_mask]
    ring_pixels = roi[ring_mask]

    if core_pixels.size == 0 or ring_pixels.size == 0:
        raise ValueError("Khong du pixel de do tuong phan cuc bo.")

    # Trung binh loi o va trung vi nen vong ngoai
    core_mean = float(np.mean(core_pixels))
    ring_median = float(np.median(ring_pixels))

    return ring_median - core_mean


def get_score(student_gray, template_gray, position):
    """
    Diem = tuong phan cuc bo tren bai hoc sinh
           - tuong phan cuc bo tren phieu mau.

    O duoc to se co loi o toi hon nen gan no.
    Bong dien thoai lam toi ca loi va nen, nen duoc giam anh huong.
    """

    x, y = position

    template_contrast = get_local_contrast(
        template_gray, x, y
    )

    student_contrast = get_local_contrast(
        student_gray, x, y
    )

    score = student_contrast - template_contrast

    return round(float(score), 1)


# ==========================================
# PHAN LOAI MOT O NHI PHAN
# ==========================================

def classify_single(score):

    if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
        return "MARKED"

    elif score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return "BLANK"


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN HAI O DAU PHAY
# ==========================================

def classify_comma(scores):

    marked = [
        i for i, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) >= 2:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    if len(marked) == 1:

        other = 1 - marked[0]

        if scores[other] < REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
            return str(marked[0] + 1)

        return "REVIEW"

    if max(scores) >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN 10 CHU SO TRONG MOT COT
# ==========================================

def classify_digit(scores):

    ranked = sorted(
        enumerate(scores),
        key=lambda item: item[1],
        reverse=True
    )

    top_digit, top_score = ranked[0]

    second_digit, second_score = ranked[1]

    # Kiem tra nhieu chu so duoc to
    marked = [
        digit
        for digit, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) > 1:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    margin = top_score - second_score

    if (
        top_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
        and margin >= DIGIT_MARGIN
    ):
        return str(top_digit)

    if top_score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN PHAN III
# ==========================================

def detect_part3(image_path):

    # Doc anh hoc sinh
    student = cv2.imread(str(image_path))

    if student is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh: {image_path}"
        )

    # Doc phieu mau
    template = cv2.imread(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))

    if template is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "Khong tim thay anh phieu mau."
        )

    if student.shape[:2] != template.shape[:2]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Kich thuoc anh khong khop phieu mau."
        )

    # Doc toa do
    with open(
        COORDINATE_PATH,
        "r",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        coordinate_data = json.load(f)

    questions = coordinate_data.get("questions", [])

    if len(questions) != 6:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Can 6 cau, hien co {len(questions)} cau."
        )

    student_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        student,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    template_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        template,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    debug = student.copy()

    results = []

    print("\nKET QUA NHAN DIEN PHAN III")
    print("=" * 70)

    # ======================================
    # XU LY TUNG CAU
    # ======================================

    for question in questions:

        q_number = question["question"]

        # ----------------------------------
        # DOC DAU AM
        # ----------------------------------

        sign_score = get_score(
            student_gray,
            template_gray,
            question["sign"]
        )

        sign_status = classify_single(
            sign_score
        )

        sign = ""

        if sign_status == "MARKED":
            sign = "-"

        # ----------------------------------
        # DOC DAU PHAY
        # ----------------------------------

        comma_scores = []

        for position in question["comma_positions"]:

            score = get_score(
                student_gray,
                template_gray,
                position
            )

            comma_scores.append(score)

        comma_result = classify_comma(
            comma_scores
        )

        # ----------------------------------
        # DOC 4 COT CHU SO
        # ----------------------------------

        digit_columns = []
        digit_scores_all = []

        for column in range(4):

            scores = []

            # Doc 10 chu so tu 0 den 9
            for digit in range(10):

                position = (
                    question["digits"][str(digit)][column]
                )

                score = get_score(
                    student_gray,
                    template_gray,
                    position
                )

                scores.append(score)

            digit_result = classify_digit(scores)

            digit_columns.append(digit_result)

            digit_scores_all.append(scores)

        # ----------------------------------
        # XAC DINH TRANG THAI CAU HOI
        # ----------------------------------

        has_multiple = (
            comma_result == "MULTIPLE"
            or "MULTIPLE" in digit_columns
        )

        has_review = (
            sign_status == "REVIEW"
            or comma_result == "REVIEW"
            or "REVIEW" in digit_columns
        )

        digit_values = [
            d for d in digit_columns
            if d not in ("", "REVIEW", "MULTIPLE")
        ]

        # Chuoi 4 cot, giu vi tri trong de kiem tra
        raw_digits = "".join(
            d if d not in ("", "REVIEW", "MULTIPLE") else "_"
            for d in digit_columns
        )

        has_digit_error = (
            "REVIEW" in digit_columns
            or "MULTIPLE" in digit_columns
        )

        if has_multiple:

            status = "MULTIPLE_MARKS"

        elif has_review:

            status = "UNCERTAIN"

        elif not digit_values:

            status = "BLANK"

        else:

            status = "VALID"

        # Chua tu dong chen dau phay vao so
        # De tranh dien giai sai vi tri dau phay
        result = {
            "question": q_number,
            "sign": sign,
            "sign_status": sign_status,
            "sign_score": sign_score,
            "comma_option": comma_result,
            "comma_scores": comma_scores,
            "digit_1": digit_columns[0],
            "digit_2": digit_columns[1],
            "digit_3": digit_columns[2],
            "digit_4": digit_columns[3],
            "raw_digits": raw_digits,
            "digit_scores": digit_scores_all,
            "status": status
        }

        results.append(result)

        print(
            f"Cau {q_number}: "
            f"{sign}{raw_digits} "
            f"| Dau phay={comma_result or 'KHONG'} "
            f"| {status}"
        )

        # ==================================
        # VE KET QUA LEN ANH
        # ==================================

        # Ve dau am
        sx, sy = map(
            int,
            map(round, question["sign"])
        )

        sign_color = (
            (0, 0, 255)
            if sign_status == "MARKED"
            else (0, 165, 255)
            if sign_status == "REVIEW"
            else (150, 150, 150)
        )

        cv2.circle(
            debug,
            (sx, sy),
            9,
            sign_color,
            2
        )

        # Ve dau phay
        for index, position in enumerate(
            question["comma_positions"]
        ):

            x, y = map(
                int,
                map(round, position)
            )

            score = comma_scores[index]

            color = (
                (0, 0, 255)
                if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                else (0, 165, 255)
                if score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD
                else (150, 150, 150)
            )

            cv2.circle(
                debug,
                (x, y),
                8,
                color,
                2
            )

        # Ve 240 o chu so
        for column in range(4):

            for digit in range(10):

                position = (
                    question["digits"][str(digit)][column]
                )

                x, y = map(
                    int,
                    map(round, position)
                )

                score = digit_scores_all[column][digit]

                if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
                    color = (0, 0, 255)

                elif score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
                    color = (0, 165, 255)

                else:
                    color = (150, 150, 150)

                cv2.circle(
                    debug,
                    (x, y),
                    7,
                    color,
                    2
                )

    # ======================================
    # THONG KE
    # ======================================

    valid = sum(
        r["status"] == "VALID"
        for r in results
    )

    blank = sum(
        r["status"] == "BLANK"
        for r in results
    )

    multiple = sum(
        r["status"] == "MULTIPLE_MARKS"
        for r in results
    )

    uncertain = sum(
        r["status"] == "UNCERTAIN"
        for r in results
    )

    # ======================================
    # LUU KET QUA
    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem

    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S"
    )

    csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part3_{timestamp}.csv"
    )

    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part3_{timestamp}.json"
    )

    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part3_debug_{timestamp}.png"
    )

    # Luu CSV
    with open(
        csv_path,
        "w",
        newline="",
        encoding="utf-8-sig"
    ) as f:

        writer = csv.DictWriter(
            f,
            fieldnames=list(results[0].keys())
        )

        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(results)

    # Luu JSON
    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            results,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    # Luu anh debug
    cv2.imwrite(
        str(debug_path),
        debug
    )

    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("THONG KE PHAN III")
    print("Nhan dang hop le:", valid)
    print("Bo trong:", blank)
    print("To nhieu o:", multiple)
    print("Can kiem tra:", uncertain)

    print("\nDA LUU:")
    print(csv_path.resolve())
    print(json_path.resolve())
    print(debug_path.resolve())


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    import argparse

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--image",
        default=str(DEFAULT_IMAGE),
        help="Anh bai lam da can chinh"
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:

        detect_part3(Path(args.image))

    except Exception as error:

        print("\nLOI:", error)

import cv2
import json
import csv
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part3_coordinates.json")

DEFAULT_IMAGE = Path(
    "output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png"
)

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

# Nguong thu nghiem
FILL_THRESHOLD = 90
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 65

# Nguong chenh lech giua hai chu so
DIGIT_MARGIN = 25

BUBBLE_RADIUS = 5


# ==========================================
# DOC DO TOI TAI TAM O TRON
# ==========================================

def get_center_value(gray, x, y):

    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))

    r = BUBBLE_RADIUS

    x1 = x - r
    y1 = y - r
    x2 = x + r + 1
    y2 = y + r + 1

    if (
        x1 < 0 or y1 < 0
        or x2 > gray.shape[1]
        or y2 > gray.shape[0]
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Toa do ngoai anh: ({x}, {y})"
        )

    roi = gray[y1:y2, x1:x2]

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r:r+1, -r:r+1]

    mask = xx**2 + yy**2 <= r**2

    return float(np.median(roi[mask]))


def get_score(student_gray, template_gray, position):

    x, y = position

    reference = get_center_value(
        template_gray, x, y
    )

    student = get_center_value(
        student_gray, x, y
    )

    return round(
        max(0, reference - student),
        1
    )


# ==========================================
# PHAN LOAI MOT O NHI PHAN
# ==========================================

def classify_single(score):

    if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
        return "MARKED"

    elif score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return "BLANK"


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN HAI O DAU PHAY
# ==========================================

        i for i, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD

    if len(marked) >= 2:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    if len(marked) == 1:

        other = 1 - marked[0]

        if scores[other] < REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
            return str(marked[0] + 1)

        return "REVIEW"

    if max(scores) >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN 10 CHU SO TRONG MOT COT
# ==========================================

def classify_digit(scores):

        key=lambda item: item[1],
        reverse=True
    )

    top_digit, top_score = ranked[0]

    second_digit, second_score = ranked[1]

    # Kiem tra nhieu chu so duoc to
    marked = [
        digit
        for digit, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) > 1:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    if (
        top_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
        and margin >= DIGIT_MARGIN

    if top_score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

        # De tranh dien giai sai vi tri dau phay

            f"| {status}"
        )

        # ==================================
        # VE KET QUA LEN ANH

        # Ve dau am
        sx, sy = map(
            int,
            map(round, question["sign"])
        )

        sign_color = (
            (0, 0, 255)
            if sign_status == "MARKED"
            else (0, 165, 255)
            if sign_status == "REVIEW"
            else (150, 150, 150)
        )

        cv2.circle(
            debug,
            (sx, sy),
            9,
            sign_color,
            2
        )

        # Ve dau phay
        for index, position in enumerate(
            question["comma_positions"]
        ):

            x, y = map(

                map(round, position)
            )

            score = comma_scores[index]

            color = (
                (0, 0, 255)
                if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                else (0, 165, 255)
                if score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD
                else (150, 150, 150)

                8,
                color,
                2

        for column in range(4):

    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem

    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S"

[22/38] TẬP TIN: archive_backup/detect_part3_shadowfix.py

import cv2
import json
import csv
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

# =========================================================
# CAU HINH PHAN III
# =========================================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part3_coordinates.json")
DEFAULT_IMAGE = Path("output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

# Nguong moi: diem duoc tinh tu high-pass + tam o da tinh chinh.
# Khong dung lai nguong 90/65 cua phien ban cu.
FILL_THRESHOLD = 16
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 10
DIGIT_MARGIN = 10

# Kich thuoc hinh hoc tren anh xem thu nghiem 1434 px rong.
# Anh trong du an duoc chuan hoa ve 1769 px rong, nen tu dong scale.
REFERENCE_PREVIEW_WIDTH = 1434.0

# Ban kinh cua vong tron va vung loi, tinh theo scale anh.
CORE_RADIUS_BASE = 3.0
RING_INNER_BASE = 7.2
RING_OUTER_BASE = 10.8
CENTER_SEARCH_BASE = 8.0
ILLUMINATION_SIGMA_BASE = 18.0

CHOICES = list(range(10))


# =========================================================
# TAO BAN DO GRADIENT DE TIM LAI TAM O TRON
# =========================================================

def build_gradient_response(gray, scale):
    """Tao phan hoi vien tron tu gradient, giam anh huong bong nen."""
    smoothed = cv2.GaussianBlur(
        gray,
        (0, 0),
        sigmaX=max(0.8, 1.0 * scale),
        sigmaY=max(0.8, 1.0 * scale)
    )

    gx = cv2.Sobel(smoothed, cv2.CV_32F, 1, 0, ksize=3)
    gy = cv2.Sobel(smoothed, cv2.CV_32F, 0, 1, ksize=3)
    magnitude = cv2.magnitude(gx, gy)

    ring_inner = RING_INNER_BASE * scale
    ring_outer = RING_OUTER_BASE * scale
    radius = int(np.ceil(ring_outer))

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-radius:radius + 1, -radius:radius + 1]
    distance = np.sqrt(xx * xx + yy * yy)

    ring_mask = (
        (distance >= ring_inner)
        & (distance <= ring_outer)
    ).astype(np.float32)

    total = float(ring_mask.sum())
    if total <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Khong tao duoc mat na vong tron.")

    kernel = ring_mask / total

    response = cv2.filter2D(
        magnitude,
        cv2.CV_32F,
        kernel,
        borderType=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT
    )

    return response


def refine_center(response, x, y, scale):
    """
    Tim lai tam o tron quanh toa do hieu chuan.
    Dung do manh gradient tren vong vien, khong dung do toi o loi.
    """
    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))
    search = int(round(CENTER_SEARCH_BASE * scale))

    h, w = response.shape

    x1 = max(0, x - search)
    x2 = min(w, x + search + 1)
    y1 = max(0, y - search)
    y2 = min(h, y + search + 1)

    patch = response[y1:y2, x1:x2]

    yy, xx = np.mgrid[y1:y2, x1:x2]

    # Uu tien tam gan toa do chuan neu hai vi tri co gradient gan nhau.
    distance_penalty = 0.4 * np.sqrt(
        (xx - x) ** 2 + (yy - y) ** 2
    )

    adjusted = patch - distance_penalty

    iy, ix = np.unravel_index(
        np.argmax(adjusted),
        adjusted.shape
    )

    return int(x1 + ix), int(y1 + iy)


# =========================================================
# BU SANG NEN VA DO MUC TO O LOI
# =========================================================

def build_ink_map(gray, scale):
    """
    Gaussian blur uoc luong nen sang thay doi cham.
    Nen - anh goc tao ban do muc toi cuc bo:
    - bong dien thoai dien rong duoc giam anh huong;
    - vet but trong loi o tao gia tri duong ro hon.
    """
    sigma = ILLUMINATION_SIGMA_BASE * scale

    background = cv2.GaussianBlur(
        gray,
        (0, 0),
        sigmaX=sigma,
        sigmaY=sigma,
        borderType=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT
    )

    ink_map = (
        background.astype(np.float32)
        - gray.astype(np.float32)
    )

    return ink_map


def disk_mean(ink_map, x, y, radius):
    x = int(x)
    y = int(y)
    r = max(1, int(round(radius)))

    h, w = ink_map.shape

    if (
        x - r < 0 or y - r < 0
        or x + r >= w or y + r >= h
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Vung do vuot ngoai anh tai ({x}, {y})."
        )

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r:r + 1, -r:r + 1]
    mask = xx * xx + yy * yy <= r * r

    patch = ink_map[
        y - r:y + r + 1,
        x - r:x + r + 1
    ]

    return float(np.mean(patch[mask]))


def get_score(
    student_ink,
    template_ink,
    student_response,
    template_response,
    position,
    scale
):
    """
    Diem = muc toi cuc bo tren bai hoc sinh
           - muc toi cuc bo tren phieu mau.

    Truoc khi do, tu dong tinh chinh tam o tren anh hoc sinh va anh mau.
    """
    x, y = position

    student_center = refine_center(
        student_response, x, y, scale
    )

    template_center = refine_center(
        template_response, x, y, scale
    )

    radius = CORE_RADIUS_BASE * scale

    student_value = disk_mean(
        student_ink,
        student_center[0],
        student_center[1],
        radius
    )

    template_value = disk_mean(
        template_ink,
        template_center[0],
        template_center[1],
        radius
    )

    score = student_value - template_value

    return (
        round(float(score), 1),
        student_center,
        template_center
    )


# =========================================================
# PHAN LOAI
# =========================================================

def classify_single(score):
    if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
        return "MARKED"
    if score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"
    return "BLANK"


def classify_comma(scores):
    marked = [
        index for index, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) >= 2:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    if len(marked) == 1:
        other = 1 - marked[0]
        if scores[other] < REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
            return str(marked[0] + 1)
        return "REVIEW"

    if max(scores) >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


def classify_digit(scores):
    ranked = sorted(
        enumerate(scores),
        key=lambda item: item[1],
        reverse=True
    )

    top_digit, top_score = ranked[0]
    second_score = ranked[1][1]

    marked = [
        digit for digit, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) > 1:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    margin = top_score - second_score

    if (
        top_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
        and margin >= DIGIT_MARGIN
    ):
        return str(top_digit)

    if top_score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


# =========================================================
# NHAN DIEN PHAN III
# =========================================================

def detect_part3(image_path):
    student = cv2.imread(str(image_path))
    if student is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh bai lam: {image_path}"
        )

    template = cv2.imread(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))
    if template is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh mau: {TEMPLATE_PATH}"
        )

    if student.shape[:2] != template.shape[:2]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Kich thuoc anh bai lam khong khop anh mau. "
            "Hay chay align_sheet.py truoc."
        )

    with open(COORDINATE_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        coordinate_data = json.load(f)

    questions = coordinate_data.get("questions", [])
    if len(questions) != 6:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Can 6 cau, hien co {len(questions)} cau."
        )

    # Anh mau du an rong 1769 px; anh trong tin nhan co the duoc
    # nen xuong 1434 px. Scale nay giu dung kich thuoc hinh hoc.
    scale = student.shape[1] / REFERENCE_PREVIEW_WIDTH

    student_gray = cv2.cvtColor(student, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    template_gray = cv2.cvtColor(template, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)

    # Ban do muc toi sau bu sang
    student_ink = build_ink_map(student_gray, scale)
    template_ink = build_ink_map(template_gray, scale)

    # Ban do gradient de tu can tam cac vong tron
    student_response = build_gradient_response(student_gray, scale)
    template_response = build_gradient_response(template_gray, scale)

    debug = student.copy()
    results = []

    print("\nKET QUA NHAN DIEN PHAN III - BU SANG + TU CAN TAM")
    print("=" * 76)
    print(
        f"Scale={scale:.3f} | Fill={FILL_THRESHOLD} | "
        f"Review={REVIEW_THRESHOLD}"
    )

    for question in questions:
        q_number = question["question"]

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # DAU AM
        # -------------------------------------------------
        sign_score, sign_center, _ = get_score(
            student_ink,
            template_ink,
            student_response,
            template_response,
            question["sign"],
            scale
        )

        sign_status = classify_single(sign_score)
        sign = "-" if sign_status == "MARKED" else ""

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # DAU PHAY
        # -------------------------------------------------
        comma_scores = []
        comma_centers = []

        for position in question["comma_positions"]:
            score, center, _ = get_score(
                student_ink,
                template_ink,
                student_response,
                template_response,
                position,
                scale
            )
            comma_scores.append(score)
            comma_centers.append(center)

        comma_result = classify_comma(comma_scores)

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # BON COT CHU SO
        # -------------------------------------------------
        digit_columns = []
        digit_scores_all = []
        digit_centers_all = []

        for column in range(4):
            scores = []
            centers = []

            for digit in range(10):
                position = question["digits"][str(digit)][column]

                score, center, _ = get_score(
                    student_ink,
                    template_ink,
                    student_response,
                    template_response,
                    position,
                    scale
                )

                scores.append(score)
                centers.append(center)

            digit_columns.append(classify_digit(scores))
            digit_scores_all.append(scores)
            digit_centers_all.append(centers)

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # TRANG THAI
        # -------------------------------------------------
        has_multiple = (
            comma_result == "MULTIPLE"
            or "MULTIPLE" in digit_columns
        )

        has_review = (
            sign_status == "REVIEW"
            or comma_result == "REVIEW"
            or "REVIEW" in digit_columns
        )

        digit_values = [
            d for d in digit_columns
            if d not in ("", "REVIEW", "MULTIPLE")
        ]

        raw_digits = "".join(
            d if d not in ("", "REVIEW", "MULTIPLE") else "_"
            for d in digit_columns
        )

        if has_multiple:
            status = "MULTIPLE_MARKS"
        elif has_review:
            status = "UNCERTAIN"
        elif not digit_values and sign_status != "MARKED" and not comma_result:
            status = "BLANK"
        elif not digit_values:
            status = "UNCERTAIN"
        else:
            status = "VALID"

        result = {
            "question": q_number,
            "sign": sign,
            "sign_status": sign_status,
            "sign_score": sign_score,
            "comma_option": comma_result,
            "comma_scores": comma_scores,
            "digit_1": digit_columns[0],
            "digit_2": digit_columns[1],
            "digit_3": digit_columns[2],
            "digit_4": digit_columns[3],
            "raw_digits": raw_digits,
            "digit_scores": digit_scores_all,
            "status": status
        }

        results.append(result)

        print(
            f"Cau {q_number}: {sign}{raw_digits} "
            f"| Dau phay={comma_result or 'KHONG'} "
            f"| {status}"
        )

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # VE ANH KIEM TRA
        # -------------------------------------------------
        def draw_point(center, color, radius):
            cv2.circle(
                debug,
                (int(center[0]), int(center[1])),
                radius,
                color,
                2
            )

        sign_color = (
            (0, 0, 255) if sign_status == "MARKED"
            else (0, 165, 255) if sign_status == "REVIEW"
            else (150, 150, 150)
        )
        draw_point(sign_center, sign_color, max(6, int(9 * scale)))

        for index, center in enumerate(comma_centers):
            score = comma_scores[index]
            color = (
                (0, 0, 255) if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                else (0, 165, 255) if score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD
                else (150, 150, 150)
            )
            draw_point(center, color, max(6, int(8 * scale)))

        for column in range(4):
            for digit in range(10):
                score = digit_scores_all[column][digit]
                center = digit_centers_all[column][digit]

                color = (
                    (0, 0, 255) if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                    else (0, 165, 255) if score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD
                    else (150, 150, 150)
                )

                draw_point(
                    center,
                    color,
                    max(5, int(7 * scale))
                )

    # =====================================================
    # THONG KE
    # =====================================================
    valid = sum(r["status"] == "VALID" for r in results)
    blank = sum(r["status"] == "BLANK" for r in results)
    multiple = sum(r["status"] == "MULTIPLE_MARKS" for r in results)
    uncertain = sum(r["status"] == "UNCERTAIN" for r in results)

    # =====================================================
    # LUU KET QUA - TEN FILE CO DONG HO
    # =====================================================
    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem
    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")

    csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part3_shadowfix_{timestamp}.csv"
    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part3_shadowfix_{timestamp}.json"
    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part3_shadowfix_debug_{timestamp}.png"

    with open(csv_path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(
            f,
            fieldnames=list(results[0].keys())
        )
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(results)

    with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(results, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)

    cv2.imwrite(str(debug_path), debug)

    print("\n" + "=" * 76)
    print("THONG KE PHAN III")
    print("Nhan dang hop le:", valid)
    print("Bo trong:", blank)
    print("To nhieu o:", multiple)
    print("Can kiem tra:", uncertain)

    print("\nDA LUU:")
    print(csv_path.resolve())
    print(json_path.resolve())
    print(debug_path.resolve())


# =========================================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# =========================================================

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--image",
        default=str(DEFAULT_IMAGE),
        help="Anh bai lam da can chinh"
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:
        detect_part3(Path(args.image))
    except Exception as error:
        print("\nLOI:", error)

import fitz
from pathlib import Path

# Tim file PDF mau trong thu muc du an
pdf_files = list(Path(".").glob("*.pdf"))

if not pdf_files:
    print("LOI: Khong tim thay file PDF mau!")
else:
    pdf_path = pdf_files[0]
    output_dir = Path("output")
    output_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    print("Dang doc file:", pdf_path.name)

    # Mo file PDF
    document = fitz.open(pdf_path)

    # Chi xu ly trang dau tien
    page = document[0]

    # Tang do phan giai anh len 3 lan
    matrix = fitz.Matrix(3, 3)

    # Chuyen trang PDF thanh anh
    pixmap = page.get_pixmap(
        matrix=matrix,
        alpha=False
    )

    # Luu anh PNG
    output_path = output_dir / "answer_sheet_master.png"
    pixmap.save(output_path)

    print("--------------------------------")
    print("CHUYEN DOI THANH CONG!")
    print("So trang PDF:", len(document))
    print("Kich thuoc anh:", pixmap.width, "x", pixmap.height)
    print("Anh duoc luu tai:", output_path.resolve())
    print("--------------------------------")

    document.close()

# test_bubble_detector.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
KIỂM CHỨNG THỰC NGHIỆM THUẬT TOÁN BUBBLE DETECTOR KHÁNG BÓNG ĐỔ
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import cv2
import numpy as np

from bubble_detector import BubbleDetector


def run_benchmark():
    detector = BubbleDetector(core_radius=9, ring_inner=17, ring_outer=22)

    part1_coord_path = Path("output/part1_coordinates.json")
    part2_coord_path = Path("output/part2_coordinates.json")

    if not part1_coord_path.exists() or not part2_coord_path.exists():
        print("[!] Lỗi: Chưa tìm thấy file tọa độ part1_coordinates.json hoặc part2_coordinates.json.")
        return

    with open(part1_coord_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        p1_data = json.load(f)["questions"]
    with open(part2_coord_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        p2_data = json.load(f)["questions"]

    test_images = [
        Path("output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png"),
        Path("output/bai_kiem_tra_02_aligned.png"),
    ]

    print("=" * 80)
    print("BENCHMARK: KIỂM CHỨNG CHỈ SỐ RELATIVE DARKNESS TRÊN DỮ LIỆU THỰC TẾ")
    print("=" * 80)

    for img_path in test_images:
        if not img_path.exists():
            print(f"[!] Bỏ qua (chưa có file): {img_path}")
            continue

        img_bgr = cv2.imread(str(img_path))
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(img_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        print(f"\n[*] Đang đánh giá: {img_path.name} (Kích thước: {gray.shape[1]}x{gray.shape[0]})")

        filled_scores = []
        blank_scores = []

        # 1. Đánh giá Phần I (40 câu) sử dụng tham số tối ưu (min_signal=0.095)
        p1_results = []
        for q_item in p1_data:
            q_num = q_item["question"]
            res = detector.classify_mcq_row(gray, q_item["choices"], min_signal=0.095, contrast_margin=0.055)
            p1_results.append((q_num, res["answer"], res["status"], res["scores"]))

            for opt, score in res["scores"].items():
                if res["answer"] == opt and res["status"] == "VALID":
                    filled_scores.append(score)
                elif res["answer"] == "":
                    blank_scores.append(score)

        print("  --- Kết quả Phần I (16 câu đầu) ---")
        for q_num, ans, status, scores in p1_results[:16]:
            print(f"  Câu {q_num:02d}: Đáp án=[{ans or ' '}] | Trạng thái={status:<11} | Scores={scores}")

        # 2. Đánh giá Phần II (8 câu x 4 ý)
        p2_results = []
        for q_item in p2_data:
            q_num = q_item["question"]
            row_res = {}
            for row_k in ["a", "b", "c", "d"]:
                true_pt = tuple(q_item["rows"][row_k]["true"])
                false_pt = tuple(q_item["rows"][row_k]["false"])
                eval_res = detector.classify_true_false_row(gray, true_pt, false_pt, min_signal=0.095, contrast_margin=0.055)
                row_res[row_k] = eval_res
                if eval_res["answer"] == "Đ":
                    filled_scores.append(eval_res["score_true"])
                    blank_scores.append(eval_res["score_false"])
                elif eval_res["answer"] == "S":
                    filled_scores.append(eval_res["score_false"])
                    blank_scores.append(eval_res["score_true"])
                else:
                    blank_scores.append(eval_res["score_true"])
                    blank_scores.append(eval_res["score_false"])

            p2_results.append((q_num, row_res))

        print("\n  --- Kết quả Phần II (Câu 1 và Câu 2) ---")
        for q_num, row_dict in p2_results[:2]:
            row_str = " | ".join([f"{k}:{v['answer'] or '-'}(T:{v['score_true']},F:{v['score_false']})" for k, v in row_dict.items()])
            print(f"  Câu {q_num}: {row_str}")

        # Thống kê phân phối
        if filled_scores and blank_scores:
            print("\n  --- THỐNG KÊ PHÂN TÁCH GIÁ TRỊ (SEPARATION MARGIN) ---")
            print(f"  Ô ĐÃ TÔ    : Min = {min(filled_scores):.3f} | Mean = {np.mean(filled_scores):.3f} | Max = {max(filled_scores):.3f}")
            print(f"  Ô BỎ TRỐNG : Min = {min(blank_scores):.3f} | Mean = {np.mean(blank_scores):.3f} | Max = {max(blank_scores):.3f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_benchmark()

          <span id="batchProgressText">Tiến độ: 0/0 (0%)</span>

          Chưa chọn bài thi.
        </div>
        <img id="gradedSheetImg" src="" alt="Bản quét phiếu bài làm đã chấm" class="hidden transition-transform origin-top duration-150 rounded shadow-2xl max-w-full" />
