  <script>
    let currentSession = null;
    let currentSubmissions = [];
    let selectedSubId = null;
    let currentSheetZoom = 1.0;
    let isBatchCancelled = false;

    window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
      await refreshSession();

      const savedSubId = localStorage.getItem("omr_active_sub_id");
      if (savedSubId && currentSubmissions.some(s => s.submission_id === savedSubId)) {
        selectSubmission(savedSubId);
      } else if (currentSubmissions.length > 0) {
        selectSubmission(currentSubmissions[0].submission_id);
      }
    });

    async function refreshSession() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/session");
        currentSession = await res.json();
        currentSubmissions = currentSession.submissions || [];

        const info = currentSession.exam_info;
        if (info) {
          document.getElementById("examHeaderTitle").innerText = `${info.subject.toUpperCase()} - ${info.period.toUpperCase()} (${info.class_name})`;
          document.getElementById("examHeaderSub").innerText = `Năm học: ${info.school_year}`;

          const badge = document.getElementById("examStatusBadge");
          if (info.is_configured) {
            badge.className = "text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-bold bg-emerald-900 text-emerald-300";
            badge.innerText = "Đã sẵn sàng chấm";
          } else {
            badge.className = "text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-bold bg-amber-900 text-amber-300";
            badge.innerText = "Chưa cấu hình đáp án";
          }
        }

        renderExamHistory(currentSession.all_exams || []);
        renderList();

        if (selectedSubId) {
          const stillExists = currentSubmissions.find(s => s.submission_id === selectedSubId);
          if (stillExists) selectSubmission(selectedSubId);
          else {
            selectedSubId = null;
            clearReviewViews();
          }
        } else if (currentSubmissions.length > 0) {
          selectSubmission(currentSubmissions[0].submission_id);
        } else {
          clearReviewViews();
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Lỗi đồng bộ dữ liệu phiên:", e);
      }
    }

    function clearReviewViews() {
      document.getElementById("reviewContainer").innerHTML = `
        <div class="text-center text-slate-400 py-36 text-xs">
          Chưa chọn bài thi.<br>Nhấn <b>+ Nạp Bài Thi</b> để bắt đầu.
        </div>`;
      const sheetImg = document.getElementById("gradedSheetImg");
      const emptyNotice = document.getElementById("sheetEmptyNotice");
      sheetImg.classList.add("hidden");
      emptyNotice.classList.remove("hidden");
      localStorage.removeItem("omr_active_sub_id");
    }

    function openModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.remove("hidden"); }
    function closeModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.add("hidden"); }

    function renderExamHistory(allExams) {
      const container = document.getElementById("examHistoryList");
      if (!allExams || allExams.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center py-8 text-xs text-slate-400">Chưa có đợt kiểm tra nào.</div>`;
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = allExams.map(ex => `
        <div class="p-3 rounded-xl border transition flex justify-between items-center ${ex.is_active ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-500 ring-1 ring-indigo-500' : 'bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border-slate-200'}">
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span class="font-bold text-xs text-slate-900">${ex.title}</span>
              ${ex.is_active ? `<span class="bg-indigo-600 text-white text-[9px] px-2 py-0.2 rounded-full font-bold">Hiện tại</span>` : ''}
            </div>
            <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
              Khởi tạo: <b>${ex.created_at || 'Mặc định'}</b> | Đã chấm: <b class="text-emerald-700">${ex.submission_count} bài</b>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            ${!ex.is_active ? `
              <button onclick="switchExamSession('${ex.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-indigo-900 hover:bg-indigo-800 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shadow">
                Mở Đợt
              </button>
            ` : ''}
            <button onclick="deleteExamSession('${ex.id}', '${ex.title}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-rose-100 hover:bg-rose-200 text-rose-700 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition" title="Xóa đợt kiểm tra này">
              Xóa
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join("");
    }

    async function deleteExamSession(examId, examTitle) {
      if (!confirm(`Xác nhận xóa đợt kiểm tra: "${examTitle}"?\nDữ liệu bài làm trong đợt này sẽ bị xóa khỏi hệ thống.`)) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/delete-exam/${examId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
        if (res.ok) {
          selectedSubId = null;
          localStorage.removeItem("omr_active_sub_id");
          await refreshSession();
        } else {
          const err = await res.json();
          alert("Lỗi: " + (err.detail || "Không thể xóa đợt này"));
        }
      } catch (e) {
        alert("Lỗi kết nối: " + e.message);
      }
    }

    async function switchExamSession(examId) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/switch-exam/${examId}`, { method: "POST" });
        if (res.ok) {
          closeModal("examHistoryModal");
          selectedSubId = null;
          localStorage.removeItem("omr_active_sub_id");
          await refreshSession();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        alert("Lỗi chuyển đợt kiểm tra: " + err.message);
      }
    }

    async function submitCreateExam() {
      const payload = {
        subject: document.getElementById("inpSubject").value,
        period: document.getElementById("inpPeriod").value,
        class_name: document.getElementById("inpClass").value,
        school_year: document.getElementById("inpYear").value
      };
      await fetch("/api/create-exam", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      closeModal("createExamModal");
      selectedSubId = null;
      localStorage.removeItem("omr_active_sub_id");
      await refreshSession();
    }

    document.getElementById("keyPicker").addEventListener("change", async (e) => {
      const file = e.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append("file", file);

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/upload-answer-key", { method: "POST", body: fd });
        const data = await res.json();
        alert(`Đã nạp đáp án thành công cho các mã đề: ${data.codes_loaded.join(", ")}.`);
        await refreshSession();
      } catch (err) {
        alert("Lỗi nạp file đáp án: " + err.message);
      }
    });

    // CHẤM HÀNG LOẠT TUẦN TỰ
    document.getElementById("filePicker").addEventListener("change", async (e) => {
      const files = Array.from(e.target.files);
      if (!files.length) return;

      e.target.value = "";

      const progressContainer = document.getElementById("batchProgressContainer");
      const progressBar = document.getElementById("batchProgressBar");
      const progressText = document.getElementById("batchProgressText");
      const currentFileText = document.getElementById("batchCurrentFile");

      progressContainer.classList.remove("hidden");
      isBatchCancelled = false;

      const totalFiles = files.length;
      let processedFiles = 0;
      let successCount = 0;
      let errorCount = 0;

      for (let i = 0; i < totalFiles; i++) {
        if (isBatchCancelled) {
          alert(`Đã dừng tiến trình chấm hàng loạt. Đã xử lý ${processedFiles}/${totalFiles} tệp.`);
          break;
        }

        const file = files[i];
        currentFileText.innerText = `Đang xử lý (${i + 1}/${totalFiles}): ${file.name}`;

        const fd = new FormData();
        fd.append("file", file);

        try {
          const res = await fetch("/api/grade-single-file", {
            method: "POST",
            body: fd
          });

          if (!res.ok) {
            const err = await res.json();
            throw new Error(err.detail || `HTTP Error: ${res.status}`);
          }

          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.new_submissions && data.new_submissions.length > 0) {
            for (let newSub of data.new_submissions) {
              const existingIdx = currentSubmissions.findIndex(s => s.submission_id === newSub.submission_id);
              if (existingIdx >= 0) {
                currentSubmissions[existingIdx] = newSub;
              } else {
                currentSubmissions.push(newSub);
              }
              if (newSub.has_error) errorCount++;
              else successCount++;
            }

            renderList();

            if (!selectedSubId && currentSubmissions.length > 0) {
              selectSubmission(currentSubmissions[currentSubmissions.length - 1].submission_id);
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error(`Lỗi chấm file ${file.name}:`, err);
          errorCount++;
        }

        processedFiles++;
        const percent = Math.round((processedFiles / totalFiles) * 100);
        progressBar.style.width = `${percent}%`;
        progressText.innerText = `Tiến độ: ${processedFiles}/${totalFiles} (${percent}%)`;
      }

      setTimeout(() => {
        progressContainer.classList.add("hidden");
        progressBar.style.width = "0%";
        if (!isBatchCancelled) {
          alert(`Đã hoàn tất chấm hàng loạt!\n- Thành công: ${successCount} bài\n- Lỗi nhận diện: ${errorCount} bài`);
        }
        refreshSession();
      }, 800);
    });

    function cancelBatchGrading() {
      isBatchCancelled = true;
    }

    function renderList() {
      const el = document.getElementById("submissionList");
      document.getElementById("badgeCount").innerText = `${currentSubmissions.length} bài`;

      if (currentSubmissions.length === 0) {
        el.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center text-slate-400 py-24 text-[11px]">Chưa có dữ liệu bài thi.</div>`;
        return;
      }

      el.innerHTML = currentSubmissions.map(sub => {
        const isSelected = sub.submission_id === selectedSubId;
        const hasWarning = sub.anomalies && sub.anomalies.length > 0;
        const hasErr = sub.has_error;

        return `
          <div onclick="selectSubmission('${sub.submission_id}')"
               class="group relative p-2 rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition border text-left ${isSelected ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-500 shadow-sm' : 'hover:bg-slate-50 border-slate-200'}">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-start">
              <span class="font-bold text-[11px] text-slate-800 truncate max-w-[120px]" title="${sub.file_name}">${sub.file_name}</span>
              <span class="font-bold text-[11px] ${hasErr ? 'text-rose-600' : (hasWarning ? 'text-amber-600' : 'text-emerald-600')}">
                ${hasErr ? 'LỖI' : (sub.scores ? sub.scores.total + ' đ' : '0 đ')}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
              <span>SBD: <b>${sub.sbd}</b> | Đề: <b>${sub.exam_code}</b></span>
              ${hasErr ? `<span class="bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 px-1 py-0.2 rounded font-semibold text-[8px]">Lỗi</span>` :
                (hasWarning ? `<span class="bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 px-1 py-0.2 rounded font-semibold text-[8px]">${sub.anomalies.length} cờ</span>` : `<span class="text-emerald-600 font-semibold text-[8px]">Hợp lệ</span>`)}
            </div>
            <button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); deleteSubmission('${sub.submission_id}', '${sub.file_name}');"
                    class="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 absolute top-1 right-1 bg-white hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 p-1 rounded border border-slate-200 shadow-sm transition"
                    title="Xóa bài thi này">
              <svg class="w-3 h-3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path></svg>
            </button>
          </div>`;
      }).join("");
    }

    function selectSubmission(subId) {
      selectedSubId = subId;
      localStorage.setItem("omr_active_sub_id", subId);
      renderList();
      const sub = currentSubmissions.find(s => s.submission_id === subId);
      if (!sub) return;

      const sheetImg = document.getElementById("gradedSheetImg");
      const emptyNotice = document.getElementById("sheetEmptyNotice");
      const versionTag = sub.updated_at ? `?v=${encodeURIComponent(sub.updated_at)}` : '';
      sheetImg.src = `/api/graded-image/${sub.submission_id}${versionTag}`;
      sheetImg.classList.remove("hidden");
      emptyNotice.classList.add("hidden");
      resetZoomSheet();

      const container = document.getElementById("reviewContainer");
      if (sub.has_error) {
        container.innerHTML = `
          <div class="p-6 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-center">
            <h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-rose-800 mb-1">Không thể nhận diện bài làm</h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-rose-600 mb-4">${sub.error_msg || 'Ảnh bài thi bị mất góc mốc định vị.'}</p>
            <button onclick="deleteSubmission('${sub.submission_id}', '${sub.file_name}')" class="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
              Xóa Bản Ghi Này
            </button>
          </div>`;
        return;
      }

      const p1 = sub.details?.part1 || {};
      const p2 = sub.details?.part2 || {};
      const p3 = sub.details?.part3 || {};

      container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center border-b pb-2.5 mb-3">
          <div>
            <h3 class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 truncate max-w-[200px]">${sub.file_name}</h3>
            <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Mã bài: ${sub.submission_id}</p>
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div class="text-right mr-1">
              <span class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 block font-bold leading-tight">TỔNG ĐIỂM</span>
              <span class="text-xl font-black text-rose-600" id="liveTotal">${sub.scores ? sub.scores.total : 0} <span class="text-[10px] font-normal text-slate-400">/ 10</span></span>
            </div>
            <button onclick="triggerInstantRegrade('${sub.submission_id}')" class="bg-indigo-900 hover:bg-indigo-800 text-white font-semibold text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg shadow">
              Cập Nhật Kết Quả
            </button>
            <button onclick="deleteSubmission('${sub.submission_id}', '${sub.file_name}')" class="bg-rose-100 hover:bg-rose-200 text-rose-700 font-bold text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition" title="Xóa bài thi này">
              Xóa
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        ${sub.anomalies && sub.anomalies.length > 0 ? `
          <div class="mb-3 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg p-2">
            <h4 class="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-900 uppercase mb-0.5">Lưu ý đối soát (${sub.anomalies.length}):</h4>
            <ul class="text-[11px] text-amber-800 list-disc list-inside space-y-0.5">
              ${sub.anomalies.map(a => `<li>${a.msg}</li>`).join("")}
            </ul>
          </div>` : ""}

        <div class="mb-3 bg-slate-50 border rounded-lg p-2">
          <label class="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase mb-1">
            Thông tin thí sinh (Đối soát viết tay)
          </label>
          <img src="/api/crop-info/${sub.submission_id}?t=${Date.now()}" 
               class="w-full max-h-40 object-contain rounded border border-slate-300 bg-white shadow-xs mb-2 transition-all hover:scale-105 origin-top" 
               alt="Thông tin viết tay thí sinh" />
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div>
              <label class="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600 mb-0.5">Số báo danh / Mã thí sinh:</label>
              <input type="text" id="editSbd" value="${sub.sbd}" class="w-full border rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-indigo-950 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-indigo-600 outline-none" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-0.5">
                <label class="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600">Mã đề thi:</label>
                <button onclick="triggerInstantRegrade('${sub.submission_id}')" class="text-[9px] bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded shadow">
                  Chấm Lại Điểm ↻
                </button>
              </div>
              <input type="text" id="editMade" value="${sub.exam_code}" 
                onchange="triggerInstantRegrade('${sub.submission_id}')"
                placeholder="Nhập 4 số mã đề..."
                class="w-full border-2 border-indigo-400 rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-indigo-950 bg-amber-50 focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-600 outline-none" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="space-y-2.5">
          <!-- Phần I -->
          <div class="border rounded-lg p-2 bg-white">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
              <span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">Phần I: Trắc nghiệm 4 lựa chọn (${Object.keys(p1).length} câu)</span>
              <span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-700" id="liveP1">${sub.scores ? sub.scores.p1 : 0} đ</span>
            </div>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-8 gap-1 text-xs">
              ${Object.entries(p1).map(([q, ans]) => `
                <div class="p-1 rounded border text-center ${ans ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-200' : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-400'}">
                  <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-400 font-mono">C${q}</div>
                  <input type="text" id="p1_q${q}" value="${ans}" maxlength="1" class="w-full text-center font-bold text-xs uppercase bg-transparent outline-none focus:bg-amber-100" />
                </div>`).join("")}
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Phần II -->
          <div class="border rounded-lg p-2 bg-white">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
              <span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">Phần II: Đúng / Sai (${Object.keys(p2).length} câu)</span>
              <span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-700" id="liveP2">${sub.scores ? sub.scores.p2 : 0} đ</span>
            </div>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 text-xs">
              ${Object.entries(p2).map(([q, rows]) => `
                <div class="border rounded p-1 bg-slate-50">
                  <div class="font-bold text-slate-700 mb-0.5 text-[10px]">Câu ${q}</div>
                  <div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1 text-center font-mono">
                    ${['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'].map(k => `
                      <div>
                        <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-400">${k}</div>
                        <input type="text" id="p2_q${q}_${k}" value="${rows[k] || ''}" maxlength="1" class="w-full text-center font-bold border rounded bg-white text-[10px] focus:bg-amber-100" />
                      </div>`).join("")}
                  </div>
                </div>`).join("")}
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Phần III -->
          <div class="border rounded-lg p-2 bg-white">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
              <span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">Phần III: Trả lời ngắn (${Object.keys(p3).length} câu)</span>
              <span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-700" id="liveP3">${sub.scores ? sub.scores.p3 : 0} đ</span>
            </div>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 text-xs">
              ${Object.entries(p3).map(([q, item]) => {
                const isErr = !item.is_valid && item.raw_string && item.raw_string !== "____";
                return `
                <div class="border rounded p-1 text-center ${isErr ? 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-300' : 'bg-slate-50'}">
                  <div class="font-bold text-slate-600 mb-0.5 text-[10px]">Câu ${q}</div>
                  <input type="number" step="any" id="p3_q${q}"
                         value="${item.is_valid && item.normalized_value !== null ? item.normalized_value : ''}"
                         placeholder="${isErr ? 'LỖI (0đ)' : ''}"
                         class="w-full border rounded text-center font-bold px-1 py-0.5 text-xs ${isErr ? 'border-rose-400 bg-white text-rose-700 placeholder-rose-500' : 'bg-white'} focus:bg-amber-100" />
                  <span class="text-[9px] block mt-0.5 font-mono ${isErr ? 'text-rose-600 font-bold' : 'text-slate-400'}">
                    Đọc: ${item.raw_string || '-'}
                  </span>
                </div>`;
              }).join("")}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    async function deleteSubmission(subId, fileName) {
      if (!confirm(`Xác nhận xóa bài thi: "${fileName}" khỏi đợt chấm?`)) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/delete-submission/${subId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
        if (res.ok) {
          if (selectedSubId === subId) {
            selectedSubId = null;
            localStorage.removeItem("omr_active_sub_id");
          }
          await refreshSession();
        } else {
          alert("Lỗi: Không thể xóa bài thi này.");
        }
      } catch (e) {
        alert("Lỗi kết nối: " + e.message);
      }
    }

    async function triggerInstantRegrade(subId) {
      const sub = currentSubmissions.find(s => s.submission_id === subId);
      if (!sub) return;

      const editMadeInput = document.getElementById("editMade");
      const editSbdInput = document.getElementById("editSbd");

      const newExamCode = editMadeInput ? editMadeInput.value.trim().padStart(4, '0') : sub.exam_code;
      const newSbd = editSbdInput ? editSbdInput.value.trim() : sub.sbd;

      const p1Data = {};
      for (let q in (sub.details?.part1 || {})) {
        const inp = document.getElementById(`p1_q${q}`);
        p1Data[q] = inp ? inp.value.trim().toUpperCase() : sub.details.part1[q];
      }

      const p2Data = {};
      for (let q in (sub.details?.part2 || {})) {
        p2Data[q] = {};
        for (let k of ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd']) {
          const inp = document.getElementById(`p2_q${q}_${k}`);
          p2Data[q][k] = inp ? inp.value.trim().toUpperCase() : (sub.details.part2[q] ? sub.details.part2[q][k] : "");
        }
      }

      const p3Data = {};
      for (let q in (sub.details?.part3 || {})) {
        const inp = document.getElementById(`p3_q${q}`);
        p3Data[q] = (inp && inp.value !== "") ? parseFloat(inp.value) : (sub.details.part3[q] ? sub.details.part3[q].normalized_value : null);
      }

      const payload = {
        submission_id: subId,
        sbd: newSbd,
        exam_code: newExamCode,
        part1: p1Data,
        part2: p2Data,
        part3: p3Data
      };

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/update-submission", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });

        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Máy chủ phản hồi lỗi khi cập nhật.");

        const data = await res.json();
        const updated = data.updated_submission;

        const idx = currentSubmissions.findIndex(s => s.submission_id === subId);
        if (idx >= 0) {
          currentSubmissions[idx] = updated;
        }

        renderList();
        selectSubmission(subId);

        const sheetImg = document.getElementById("gradedSheetImg");
        if (sheetImg) {
          sheetImg.src = `/api/graded-image/${subId}?t=${Date.now()}`;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        alert("Lỗi khi chấm lại điểm: " + err.message);
      }
    }

    function zoomSheet(delta) {
      currentSheetZoom = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(2.5, currentSheetZoom + delta));
      document.getElementById("gradedSheetImg").style.transform = `scale(${currentSheetZoom})`;
    }
    function resetZoomSheet() {
      currentSheetZoom = 1.0;
      document.getElementById("gradedSheetImg").style.transform = "scale(1.0)";
    }
    function openGradedTab() {
      if (selectedSubId) {
        window.open(`/api/graded-image/${selectedSubId}`, "_blank");
      }
    }

    function downloadTemplate() { window.location.href = "/api/download-template"; }

    // =========================================================================
    // HỘP THOẠI CHỌN NƠI LƯU (SAVE AS DIALOG) TÍCH HỢP CHO PDF VÀ EXCEL
    // =========================================================================
    async function saveFileWithPicker(fileType) {
      const isPdf = fileType === 'pdf';
      const btn = document.getElementById(isPdf ? "btnExportPdf" : "btnExportExcel");
      const oldHtml = btn.innerHTML;
      btn.innerHTML = `<span class="inline-block animate-spin mr-1">⏳</span> Đang tạo...`;
      btn.disabled = true;

      const endpoint = isPdf ? "/api/export-graded-pdf" : "/api/export-excel";
      const mimeType = isPdf 
        ? "application/pdf" 
        : "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet";
      const ext = isPdf ? ".pdf" : ".xlsx";
      const desc = isPdf ? "Tập phiếu đã chấm PDF" : "Bảng điểm Excel";

      try {
        const res = await fetch(endpoint);
        if (!res.ok) {
          const errData = await res.json().catch(() => ({ detail: "Mã lỗi HTTP: " + res.status }));
          throw new Error(errData.detail || "Không thể xuất tệp dữ liệu.");
        }

        // Lấy tên tệp gợi ý từ header Content-Disposition
        let suggestedName = isPdf ? `TAP_PHIEU_DA_CHAM_${Date.now()}.pdf` : `BANG_DIEM_${Date.now()}.xlsx`;
        const disposition = res.headers.get("content-disposition");
        if (disposition && disposition.indexOf("filename=") !== -1) {
          const matches = /filename[^;=\n]*=((['"]).*?\2|[^;\n]*)/.exec(disposition);
          if (matches != null && matches[1]) {
            suggestedName = matches[1].replace(/['"]/g, '');
          }
        }

        const blob = await res.blob();

        // 1. NẾU TRÌNH DUYỆT HỖ TRỢ FILE SYSTEM ACCESS API: MỞ CỬA SỔ CHỌN NƠI LƯU (SAVE AS)
        if ('showSaveFilePicker' in window) {
          try {
            const handle = await window.showSaveFilePicker({
              suggestedName: suggestedName,
              types: [{
                description: desc,
                accept: { [mimeType]: [ext] }
              }]
            });
            const writable = await handle.createWritable();
            await writable.write(blob);
            await writable.close();
            alert(`[✓] Đã lưu tệp thành công vào vị trí bạn đã chọn!`);
            return;
          } catch (pickerErr) {
            // Nếu người dùng chủ động nhấn 'Cancel' trên cửa sổ Save As
            if (pickerErr.name === 'AbortError') {
              return;
            }
            console.warn("showSaveFilePicker thất bại, chuyển sang chế độ tải trực tiếp:", pickerErr);
          }
        }

        // 2. PHƯƠNG ÁN DỰ PHÒNG AN TOÀN (FALLBACK)
        const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement("a");
        a.style.display = "none";
        a.href = url;
        a.download = suggestedName;
        document.body.appendChild(a);
        a.click();
        document.body.removeChild(a);
        window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

      } catch (err) {
        alert("Thông báo xuất tệp: " + err.message);
      } finally {
        btn.innerHTML = oldHtml;
        btn.disabled = false;
      }
    }

    // SMART AUTO-POLLING TỪ THIẾT BỊ DI ĐỘNG (DUY NHẤT 1 VÒNG LẶP)
    setInterval(async () => {
      const activeEl = document.activeElement;
      if (activeEl && (activeEl.tagName === "INPUT" || activeEl.tagName === "TEXTAREA")) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/session");
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();
        const serverSubs = data.submissions || [];

        if (serverSubs.length !== currentSubmissions.length) {
          const isNewAdded = serverSubs.length > currentSubmissions.length;
          currentSubmissions = serverSubs;
          renderList();

          if (isNewAdded && currentSubmissions.length > 0) {
            const newestSub = currentSubmissions[currentSubmissions.length - 1];
            selectSubmission(newestSub.submission_id);

            const badge = document.getElementById("badgeCount");
            badge.className = "text-[10px] bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full animate-bounce";
            setTimeout(() => {
              badge.className = "text-[10px] bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full";
            }, 1800);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // Bỏ qua lỗi ngắt quãng mạng
      }
    }, 2000);
  </script>
</body>
</html>

      <div id="reticle-bl" class="target-reticle reticle-red bottom-2 left-2 rounded-xl"></div>

      <div class="w-full text-center text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono tracking-widest mt-1">MỐC TRÊN</div>
      
      <!-- HỘP HƯỚNG DẪN ĐIỀU HƯỚNG -->
      <div id="guidanceBox" class="text-center text-xs font-bold text-amber-300 bg-black/85 px-4 py-2 rounded-full mx-auto shadow-xl border border-amber-500/50 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5">
        <span id="guideIcon">🔍</span>
        <span id="guideText">Đưa 4 góc bài thi vào 4 ô vuông ngắm</span>
      </div>

      <div class="w-full text-center text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono tracking-widest mb-1">MỐC DƯỚI</div>
    </div>
  </main>

  <!-- FOOTER -->
  <footer class="w-full px-6 py-4 bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur z-30 flex justify-around items-center shrink-0 border-t border-slate-800">
    <button onclick="switchCamera()" class="p-3 bg-slate-800 rounded-full text-slate-300 border border-slate-700" title="Đổi camera">
      <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 2A8.001 8.001 0 004.582 9m0 0H9m11 11v-5h-.581m0 0a8.003 8.003 0 01-15.357-2m15.357 2H15"></path></svg>
    </button>

    <!-- Nút chụp tay (bấm chụp bất kỳ lúc nào nếu thấy góc đã khớp) -->
    <button id="snapBtn" onclick="triggerManualCapture()" class="w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-4 border-white bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 active:scale-95 transition flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/40">
      <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-full border-2 border-black/20 bg-white"></div>
    </button>

    <button onclick="toggleTorch()" class="p-3 bg-slate-800 rounded-full text-slate-300 border border-slate-700" title="Đèn Flash">
      <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13 10V3L4 14h7v7l9-11h-7z"></path></svg>
    </button>
  </footer>

  <canvas id="analysisCanvas" class="hidden"></canvas>
  <canvas id="captureCanvas" class="hidden"></canvas>

  <script>
    let currentStream = null;
    let videoTrack = null;
    let facingMode = "environment";
    let isGrading = false;
    let lockFrames = 0;

    const RETICLES = [
      { id: "reticle-tl", name: "Trên-Trái", matched: false },
      { id: "reticle-tr", name: "Trên-Phải", matched: false },
      { id: "reticle-bl", name: "Dưới-Trái", matched: false },
      { id: "reticle-br", name: "Dưới-Phải", matched: false }
    ];

    window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
      await loadSessionInfo();
      await startCamera();
      setInterval(runOpticalMarkerDetection, 120);
    });

    async function loadSessionInfo() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/session");
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.exam_info) {
          document.getElementById("examTitle").innerText = `${data.exam_info.subject} - LỚP ${data.exam_info.class_name}`;
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    async function startCamera() {
      if (currentStream) currentStream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
      try {
        currentStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
          video: { facingMode: { ideal: facingMode }, width: { ideal: 1920 }, height: { ideal: 1080 } },
          audio: false
        });
        const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
        video.srcObject = currentStream;
        videoTrack = currentStream.getVideoTracks()[0];
      } catch (err) {
        alert("Lỗi Camera: " + err.message);
      }
    }

    function switchCamera() {
      facingMode = (facingMode === "environment") ? "user" : "environment";
      startCamera();
    }

    async function toggleTorch() {
      if (videoTrack && videoTrack.getCapabilities && videoTrack.getCapabilities().torch) {
        const s = videoTrack.getSettings();
        await videoTrack.applyConstraints({ advanced: [{ torch: !s.torch }] });
      }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // THUẬT TOÁN ĐO TƯƠNG PHẢN ĐỘNG & TÍNH TOÁN CHỈ DẪN KHÔNG GIAN
    // =========================================================================
    function runOpticalMarkerDetection() {
      if (isGrading) return;

      const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
      if (!video || video.readyState !== 4) return;

      const vw = video.videoWidth;
      const vh = video.videoHeight;
      const cw = video.clientWidth;
      const ch = video.clientHeight;
      if (vw === 0 || vh === 0 || cw === 0 || ch === 0) return;

      const scale = Math.max(cw / vw, ch / vh);
      const offsetX = (cw - vw * scale) / 2;
      const offsetY = (ch - vh * scale) / 2;

      const canvas = document.getElementById("analysisCanvas");
      canvas.width = 36;
      canvas.height = 36;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });

      let matchedCount = 0;

      RETICLES.forEach((reticle) => {
        const el = document.getElementById(reticle.id);
        const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();

        const sampleW = Math.round((rect.width / scale) * 1.3);
        const sampleH = Math.round((rect.height / scale) * 1.3);
        const vidX = Math.round((rect.left + rect.width / 2 - offsetX) / scale);
        const vidY = Math.round((rect.top + rect.height / 2 - offsetY) / scale);

        const sx = Math.max(0, Math.min(vw - sampleW, vidX - sampleW / 2));
        const sy = Math.max(0, Math.min(vh - sampleH, vidY - sampleH / 2));

        ctx.drawImage(video, sx, sy, sampleW, sampleH, 0, 0, 36, 36);
        const imgData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, 36, 36).data;

        let minLum = 255, maxLum = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < imgData.length; i += 4) {
          const lum = 0.299 * imgData[i] + 0.587 * imgData[i + 1] + 0.114 * imgData[i + 2];
          if (lum < minLum) minLum = lum;
          if (lum > maxLum) maxLum = lum;
        }

        const contrast = maxLum - minLum;
        const thresh = minLum + contrast * 0.45;
        let darkPixels = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < imgData.length; i += 4) {
          const lum = 0.299 * imgData[i] + 0.587 * imgData[i + 1] + 0.114 * imgData[i + 2];
          if (lum <= thresh) darkPixels++;
        }

        // Nhận diện mốc đen nhạy: chỉ cần có mốc xuất hiện trong ô vuông
        const isMatched = (contrast >= 24.0 && darkPixels >= 18 && darkPixels <= 950);
        reticle.matched = isMatched;

        if (isMatched) {
          el.classList.remove("reticle-red");
          el.classList.add("reticle-green");
          matchedCount++;
        } else {
          el.classList.remove("reticle-green");
          el.classList.add("reticle-red");
        }
      });

      updateGuidanceUI(matchedCount);
    }

    function updateGuidanceUI(matchedCount) {
      const matchBadge = document.getElementById("matchCounter");
      const guideBox = document.getElementById("guidanceBox");
      const guideText = document.getElementById("guideText");
      const guideIcon = document.getElementById("guideIcon");
      const viewfinder = document.getElementById("viewfinder");

      matchBadge.innerText = `Mốc: ${matchedCount}/4`;

      const tl = RETICLES[0].matched;
      const tr = RETICLES[1].matched;
      const bl = RETICLES[2].matched;
      const br = RETICLES[3].matched;

      if (matchedCount === 4) {
        matchBadge.className = "px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-900 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500 font-mono";
        viewfinder.className = "relative z-10 w-[86%] max-w-[360px] aspect-[1/1.414] border-2 border-emerald-400 rounded-2xl viewfinder-mask shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(16,185,129,0.7)] transition-all flex flex-col justify-between p-2";
        guideBox.className = "text-center text-xs font-bold text-emerald-200 bg-emerald-950/95 px-4 py-2 rounded-full mx-auto shadow-xl border border-emerald-500 animate-pulse flex items-center gap-1.5";
        guideIcon.innerText = "✓";
        guideText.innerText = "ĐÃ KHÓA 4 GÓC! Đang chụp chấm...";

        lockFrames++;
        if (lockFrames >= 2) {
          executeGradingProcess();
        }
        return;
      }

      lockFrames = 0;
      matchBadge.className = "px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-rose-950 text-rose-300 border border-rose-800 font-mono";
      viewfinder.className = "relative z-10 w-[86%] max-w-[360px] aspect-[1/1.414] border-2 border-red-500/70 rounded-2xl viewfinder-mask transition-all flex flex-col justify-between p-2";
      guideBox.className = "text-center text-xs font-bold text-amber-300 bg-black/85 px-4 py-2 rounded-full mx-auto shadow-xl border border-amber-500/50 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5";

      // ĐƯA RA CHỈ DẪN KHÔNG GIAN DỰA THEO CÁC GÓC BỊ THIẾU
      if (tl && bl && !tr && !br) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "➔";
        guideText.innerText = "Dịch camera sang PHẢI một chút";
      } else if (tr && br && !tl && !bl) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "⬅";
        guideText.innerText = "Dịch camera sang TRÁI một chút";
      } else if (tl && tr && !bl && !br) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "⬇";
        guideText.innerText = "Dịch camera XUỐNG DƯỚI";
      } else if (bl && br && !tl && !tr) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "⬆";
        guideText.innerText = "Dịch camera LÊN TRÊN";
      } else if (matchedCount === 1) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "📐";
        guideText.innerText = "Cầm máy SONG SONG với mặt bàn";
      } else if (matchedCount === 0) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "🔍";
        guideText.innerText = "Đưa 4 góc bài thi vào 4 ô vuông đỏ";
      } else {
        guideIcon.innerText = "⚡";
        guideText.innerText = `Đã khớp ${matchedCount}/4. Bấm nút tròn để chụp ngay!`;
      }
    }

    function triggerManualCapture() {
      if (!isGrading) executeGradingProcess();
    }

    async function executeGradingProcess() {
      isGrading = true;
      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(100);

      const guideText = document.getElementById("guideText");
      const guideIcon = document.getElementById("guideIcon");
      guideIcon.innerText = "⏳";
      guideText.innerText = "Đang nắn phẳng & chấm điểm...";

      const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
      const canvas = document.getElementById("captureCanvas");
      canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
      canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
      canvas.getContext("2d").drawImage(video, 0, 0);

      canvas.toBlob(async (blob) => {
        const fd = new FormData();
        fd.append("file", blob, `Mobile_${Date.now()}.jpg`);

        try {
          const res = await fetch("/api/grade-single-file", { method: "POST", body: fd });
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.new_submissions && data.new_submissions.length > 0) {
            const sub = data.new_submissions[0];
            if (sub.has_error) {
              if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([100, 80, 100]);
              alert("Ảnh chụp hơi nghiêng hoặc thiếu sáng khiến máy tính chưa khớp mốc.\nHãy giữ máy song song với mặt bàn và chụp lại.");
            } else {
              if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([60, 40, 150]);
              alert(`✓ CHẤM THÀNH CÔNG!\n────────────────\n- Số báo danh : ${sub.sbd}\n- Mã đề thi   : ${sub.exam_code}\n- TỔNG ĐIỂM   : ${sub.scores.total} / 10\n\n(Bài thi đã tự động hiển thị trên máy tính)`);
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          alert("Lỗi kết nối máy chủ: " + err.message);
        } finally {
          isGrading = false;
          lockFrames = 0;
        }
      }, "image/jpeg", 0.95);
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

import cv2
from pathlib import Path

# Anh phieu mau
IMAGE_PATH = Path("output") / "answer_sheet_master.png"

# Doc anh
image = cv2.imread(str(IMAGE_PATH))

if image is None:
    print("LOI: Khong tim thay anh phieu mau!")
    exit()

# Hien thi anh
display_image = image.copy()

print("HUONG DAN:")
print("1. Click chuot vao tam o dap an A cua cau 1")
print("2. Click chuot vao tam o dap an B cua cau 1")
print("3. Click chuot vao tam o dap an C cua cau 1")
print("4. Click chuot vao tam o dap an D cua cau 1")
print("5. Nhan phim ESC de ket thuc")

points = []


def mouse_callback(event, x, y, flags, param):
    if event == cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        points.append((x, y))

        cv2.circle(
            display_image,
            (x, y),
            8,
            (0, 255, 0),
            -1
        )

        cv2.putText(
            display_image,
            str(len(points)),
            (x + 10, y),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.7,
            (0, 0, 255),
            2
        )

        print(f"Diem {len(points)}: x={x}, y={y}")

        # Thu nho anh de vua man hinh

# Thu nho anh de vua man hinh
screen_width = 1000
screen_height = 700

h, w = image.shape[:2]

scale = min(
    screen_width / w,
    screen_height / h,
    1.0
)

small_image = cv2.resize(
    image,
    None,
    fx=scale,
    fy=scale,
    interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA
)

# Hien thi anh voi cua so co the thay doi kich thuoc

    "HIEU CHINH TOA DO",
    cv2.WINDOW_NORMAL
)

cv2.imshow("HIEU CHINH TOA DO", small_image)

cv2.resizeWindow(
    "HIEU CHINH TOA DO",
    screen_width,
    screen_height
)



cv2.namedWindow("HIEU CHINH TOA DO")
cv2.setMouseCallback("HIEU CHINH TOA DO", mouse_callback)

while True:
    cv2.imshow("HIEU CHINH TOA DO", display_image)

    key = cv2.waitKey(1) & 0xFF

    if key == 27:  # ESC
        break

cv2.destroyAllWindows()

print("\nTOA DO CAC DIEM DA CHON:")
print(points)

# calibrate_header.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
HIEU CHUAN SO BAO DANH (8 COT) VA MA DE THI (4 COT)
Theo chuan phoi answer_sheet_master.png (1769 x 2526 px)
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import cv2
import numpy as np

IMAGE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

# Quy cách:
# Số báo danh: 8 cột x 10 hàng (0..9) = 80 ô
# Mã đề thi: 4 cột x 10 hàng (0..9) = 40 ô
# Tổng cộng: 12 cột x 10 hàng = 120 ô

CLICK_LABELS = [
    # 1. Hàng chữ số 0 của SBD (8 điểm: Cột 1 đến Cột 8)
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 1",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 2",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 3",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 4",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 5",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 6",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 7",
    "SBD - Chu so 0 - Cot 8",
    # 2. Hàng chữ số 0 của Mã đề (4 điểm: Cột 1 đến Cột 4)
    "Ma de - Chu so 0 - Cot 1",
    "Ma de - Chu so 0 - Cot 2",
    "Ma de - Chu so 0 - Cot 3",
    "Ma de - Chu so 0 - Cot 4",
    # 3. Điểm đáy để tính khoảng cách bước nhảy hàng dọc (Hàng số 9)
    "SBD - Chu so 9 - Cot 1"
]

image = cv2.imread(str(IMAGE_PATH))
if image is None:
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Khong tim thay anh: {IMAGE_PATH}")

height, width = image.shape[:2]

scale = min(1100 / width, 780 / height, 1.0)
display_width = int(width * scale)
display_height = int(height * scale)
scale_x = display_width / width
scale_y = display_height / height

display_original = cv2.resize(image, (display_width, display_height), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
display_image = display_original.copy()
points = []

WINDOW_NAME = "HIEU CHUAN SO BAO DANH VA MA DE"


def redraw():
    global display_image
    display_image = display_original.copy()
    for index, (x, y) in enumerate(points):
        px = int(round(x * scale_x))
        py = int(round(y * scale_y))
        cv2.circle(display_image, (px, py), 5, (0, 255, 0), 2)
        cv2.putText(
            display_image,
            str(index + 1),
            (px + 6, py - 6),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.4,
            (0, 0, 255),
            1
        )
    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)


def mouse_callback(event, x, y, flags, param):
    if event != cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        return

    if len(points) >= len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print("Da chon du 13 diem. Nhan phím S de luu toa do.")
        return

    orig_x = int(round(x / scale_x))
    orig_y = int(round(y / scale_y))
    points.append((orig_x, orig_y))
    idx = len(points) - 1

    print(f"[{idx + 1}/13] {CLICK_LABELS[idx]}: x={orig_x}, y={orig_y}")
    redraw()

    if len(points) < len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print(f"--> Tiep theo: {CLICK_LABELS[len(points)]}")
    else:
        print("\n[✓] DA CHON DU 13 DIEM! Nhan phim S de luu, Backspace de xoa diem vua chon.")


def save_calibration():
    if len(points) != len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print(f"Chua du 13 diem: {len(points)}/13")
        return

    # 8 tọa độ X của SBD (Cột 1 đến 8)
    sbd_xs = [float(points[i][0]) for i in range(8)]
    # 4 tọa độ X của Mã đề (Cột 1 đến 4)
    made_xs = [float(points[i][0]) for i in range(8, 12)]

    # Hàng chữ số 0: lấy trung bình Y của 12 điểm chữ số 0
    row_zero_y = float(np.mean([points[i][1] for i in range(12)]))
    # Điểm chữ số 9 của cột 1
    row_nine_y = float(points[12][1])

    row_step = (row_nine_y - row_zero_y) / 9.0

    if row_step <= 10.0:
        raise ValueError(f"Khoang cach hang khong hop le: {row_step:.2f}")

    # Sinh danh sách tọa độ 10 hàng chữ số
    digits_y = [round(row_zero_y + d * row_step, 2) for d in range(10)]

    sbd_grid = []
    for col_idx, cx in enumerate(sbd_xs, start=1):
        col_data = {"column": col_idx, "x": round(cx, 2), "digits": {}}
        for d in range(10):
            col_data["digits"][str(d)] = [round(cx, 2), digits_y[d]]
        sbd_grid.append(col_data)

    made_grid = []
    for col_idx, cx in enumerate(made_xs, start=1):
        col_data = {"column": col_idx, "x": round(cx, 2), "digits": {}}
        for d in range(10):
            col_data["digits"][str(d)] = [round(cx, 2), digits_y[d]]
        made_grid.append(col_data)

    header_result = {
        "template": "answer_sheet_master",
        "template_width": width,
        "template_height": height,
        "row_step": round(row_step, 3),
        "digits_y_rows": digits_y,
        "sbd_columns": sbd_grid,
        "made_columns": made_grid
    }

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "header_coordinates.json"
    with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(header_result, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)

    # Vẽ kiểm tra 120 ô tròn lên ảnh mẫu
    preview = image.copy()
    for col in sbd_grid:
        for d, (x, y) in col["digits"].items():
            cv2.circle(preview, (int(x), int(y)), 8, (0, 200, 0), 2)
            cv2.putText(preview, d, (int(x) - 4, int(y) + 4), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.35, (0, 0, 255), 1)

    for col in made_grid:
        for d, (x, y) in col["digits"].items():
            cv2.circle(preview, (int(x), int(y)), 8, (255, 100, 0), 2)
            cv2.putText(preview, d, (int(x) - 4, int(y) + 4), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.35, (0, 0, 255), 1)

    preview_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "header_calibration_preview.png"
    cv2.imwrite(str(preview_path), preview)

    print("\n========================================================")
    print("HIEU CHUAN SO BAO DANH & MA DE THANH CONG!")
    print(f"- Khoang cach hang doc (row_step): {row_step:.2f} px")
    print(f"- File toa do da luu: {json_path.resolve()}")
    print(f"- Anh preview kiem tra: {preview_path.resolve()}")
    print("========================================================")


print("========================================================")
print("HUONG DAN HIEU CHUAN HEADER (SO BAO DANH & MA DE THI)")
print("========================================================")
for i, lbl in enumerate(CLICK_LABELS, start=1):
    print(f"{i:2d}. Click vao TAM o: {lbl}")
print("\n- Phim S: Luu toa do sau khi click du 13 diem")
print("- Phim Backspace: Xoa diem vua click nham")
print("- Phim ESC: Huy bo")

cv2.namedWindow(WINDOW_NAME, cv2.WINDOW_NORMAL)
cv2.resizeWindow(WINDOW_NAME, display_width, display_height)
cv2.setMouseCallback(WINDOW_NAME, mouse_callback)

while True:
    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)
    key = cv2.waitKey(20) & 0xFF
    if key == 27:
        print("Da huy.")
        break
    elif key in (8, 127):  # Backspace / Delete
        if points:
            removed = points.pop()
            print(f"Da xoa diem: {removed}")
            redraw()
    elif key == ord("s"):
        try:
            save_calibration()
            break
        except Exception as err:
            print("LOI:", err)

cv2.destroyAllWindows()

[34/38] TẬP TIN: tools/calibration/calibrate_part1.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import cv2
import json
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
import config

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

IMAGE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

# Lay 4 toa do cau 1 da hieu chuan
FIRST_QUESTION = getattr(
    config,
    "PART1_Q1_POINTS",
    getattr(config, "ANSWER_POINTS", None)
)

if FIRST_QUESTION is None or len(FIRST_QUESTION) != 4:
    raise ValueError(
        "Config.py phai co PART1_Q1_POINTS hoac ANSWER_POINTS gom 4 toa do."
    )

FIRST_QUESTION = [
    tuple(map(float, point))
    for point in FIRST_QUESTION
]

# 13 diem can click them
LABELS = [
    "Cau 11 - A", "Cau 11 - B",
    "Cau 11 - C", "Cau 11 - D",

    "Cau 21 - A", "Cau 21 - B",
    "Cau 21 - C", "Cau 21 - D",

    "Cau 31 - A", "Cau 31 - B",
    "Cau 31 - C", "Cau 31 - D",

    "Cau 10 - A"
]

CHOICES = ["A", "B", "C", "D"]

# ==========================================
# DOC ANH
# ==========================================

image = cv2.imread(str(IMAGE_PATH))

if image is None:
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Khong tim thay anh: {IMAGE_PATH}"
    )

height, width = image.shape[:2]

# Thu nho anh vua man hinh
scale = min(
    1050 / width,
    760 / height,
    1.0
)

display_width = int(width * scale)
display_height = int(height * scale)

scale_x = display_width / width
scale_y = display_height / height

display_image = cv2.resize(
    image,
    (display_width, display_height),
    interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA
)

# Danh sach 13 diem vua click
clicked_points = []

window_name = "HIEU CHUAN 40 CAU PHAN I"

# Ve 4 diem cau 1 da co san
for i, (x, y) in enumerate(FIRST_QUESTION):

    px = int(round(x * scale_x))
    py = int(round(y * scale_y))

    cv2.circle(
        display_image, (px, py), 6,
        (255, 0, 0), 2
    )

    cv2.putText(
        display_image,
        f"1{CHOICES[i]}",
        (px + 7, py - 5),
        cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
        0.4,
        (255, 0, 0),
        1
    )


def mouse_callback(event, x, y, flags, param):

    if event != cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        return

    if len(clicked_points) >= len(LABELS):
        print("Da chon du 13 diem.")
        return

    # Chuyen toa do man hinh ve toa do anh goc
    original_x = int(round(x / scale_x))
    original_y = int(round(y / scale_y))

    clicked_points.append(
        (original_x, original_y)
    )

    index = len(clicked_points)
    label = LABELS[index - 1]

    cv2.circle(
        display_image,
        (x, y),
        6,
        (0, 255, 0),
        2
    )

    cv2.putText(
        display_image,
        str(index),
        (x + 7, y - 5),
        cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
        0.45,
        (0, 0, 255),
        1
    )

    cv2.imshow(window_name, display_image)

    print(
        f"{index}/13 - {label}: "
        f"x={original_x}, y={original_y}"
    )

    if index < len(LABELS):
        print("Tiep theo:", LABELS[index])


# ==========================================
# HUONG DAN VA HIEU CHUAN
# ==========================================

print("====================================")
print("HIEU CHUAN 40 CAU PHAN I")
print("====================================")
print("4 diem cua cau 1 da duoc nap san.")
print("Hay click vao TAM cac o tron theo thu tu:")
for i, label in enumerate(LABELS, start=1):
    print(f"{i}. {label}")

print("\nSau khi chon du 13 diem, nhan phim S de luu.")
print("Nhan ESC neu muon huy.")

cv2.namedWindow(
    window_name,
    cv2.WINDOW_NORMAL
)

cv2.resizeWindow(
    window_name,
    display_width,
    display_height
)

cv2.setMouseCallback(
    window_name,
    mouse_callback
)

while True:

    cv2.imshow(window_name, display_image)

    key = cv2.waitKey(20) & 0xFF

    if key == 27:
        print("Da huy hieu chuan.")
        break

    if key == ord("s"):

        if len(clicked_points) != len(LABELS):
            print(
                f"Chua du diem: "
                f"{len(clicked_points)}/13"
            )
            continue

        # ==================================
        # TINH KHOANG CACH CAC HANG
        # ==================================

        groups = [
            FIRST_QUESTION,
            clicked_points[0:4],
            clicked_points[4:8],
            clicked_points[8:12]
        ]

        y_starts = [
            float(np.mean([p[1] for p in group]))
            for group in groups
        ]

        # Cau 10 la hang thu 10 cua khoi dau
        y_step = (
            clicked_points[12][1] - y_starts[0]
        ) / 9

        if y_step <= 0:
            print("LOI: Khoang cach hang khong hop le.")
            continue

        # ==================================
        # TAO TOA DO 160 O
        # ==================================

        questions = []

        for block_index, group in enumerate(groups):

            x_positions = [
                float(point[0])
                for point in group
            ]

            for row in range(10):

                question_number = (
                    block_index * 10 + row + 1
                )

                y = y_starts[block_index] + row * y_step

                choices = {}

                for choice_index, choice in enumerate(CHOICES):

                    choices[choice] = [
                        round(x_positions[choice_index], 2),
                        round(y, 2)
                    ]

                questions.append({
                    "question": question_number,
                    "choices": choices
                })

        result = {
            "template": "answer_sheet_v1",
            "template_width": width,
            "template_height": height,
            "row_step": round(y_step, 3),
            "questions": questions
        }

        OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

        json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "part1_coordinates.json"

        with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            json.dump(
                result,
                file,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                indent=4
            )

        # ==================================
        # VE KIEM TRA 160 TOA DO
        # ==================================

        preview = image.copy()

        for question in questions:

            q = question["question"]

            for choice, point in question["choices"].items():

                x, y = map(int, point)

                cv2.circle(
                    preview,
                    (x, y),
                    7,
                    (0, 180, 0),
                    2
                )

                cv2.putText(
                    preview,
                    str(q),
                    (x + 7, y - 5),
                    cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                    0.3,
                    (0, 0, 255),
                    1
                )

        preview_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "part1_calibration_preview.png"

        cv2.imwrite(
            str(preview_path),
            preview
        )

        print("\nHIEU CHUAN THANH CONG!")
        print("So cau:", len(questions))
        print("So o dap an:", len(questions) * 4)
        print("Khoang cach hang:", round(y_step, 2))
        print("File toa do:", json_path.resolve())
        print("Anh kiem tra:", preview_path.resolve())

        break

cv2.destroyAllWindows()

[35/38] TẬP TIN: tools/calibration/calibrate_part2.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import cv2
import json
from pathlib import Path

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

IMAGE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

ROWS = ["a", "b", "c", "d"]

# Tao danh sach 17 diem can click
CLICK_LABELS = [
    "Cau 1 - y a - DUNG",
    "Cau 1 - y a - SAI",
    "Cau 1 - y d - DUNG",
]

for q in range(2, 9):
    CLICK_LABELS.extend([
        f"Cau {q} - y a - DUNG",
        f"Cau {q} - y a - SAI",
    ])

# ==========================================
# DOC ANH PHIEU MAU
# ==========================================

image = cv2.imread(str(IMAGE_PATH))

if image is None:
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Khong tim thay anh: {IMAGE_PATH}"
    )

height, width = image.shape[:2]

# Tu dong thu nho anh vua cua so
screen_width = 1100
screen_height = 800

scale = min(
    screen_width / width,
    screen_height / height,
    1.0
)

display_width = int(width * scale)
display_height = int(height * scale)

scale_x = display_width / width
scale_y = display_height / height

display_image = cv2.resize(
    image,
    (display_width, display_height),
    interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA
)

original_display = display_image.copy()

points = []

WINDOW_NAME = "HIEU CHUAN PHAN II"


# ==========================================
# VE CAC DIEM DA CHON
# ==========================================

def redraw():

    global display_image

    display_image = original_display.copy()

    for index, (x, y) in enumerate(points):

        px = int(round(x * scale_x))
        py = int(round(y * scale_y))

        cv2.circle(
            display_image,
            (px, py),
            5,
            (0, 255, 0),
            2
        )

        cv2.putText(
            display_image,
            str(index + 1),
            (px + 5, py - 5),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.4,
            (0, 0, 255),
            1
        )

    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)


# ==========================================
# XU LY CLICK CHUOT
# ==========================================

def mouse_callback(event, x, y, flags, param):

    if event != cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        return

    if len(points) >= len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print("Da chon du 17 diem.")
        return

    # Chuyen toa do hien thi ve toa do anh goc
    original_x = int(round(x / scale_x))
    original_y = int(round(y / scale_y))

    points.append((original_x, original_y))

    index = len(points) - 1

    print(
        f"{index + 1}/17 - "
        f"{CLICK_LABELS[index]}: "
        f"x={original_x}, y={original_y}"
    )

    redraw()

    if len(points) < len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print(
            "Tiep theo:",
            CLICK_LABELS[len(points)]
        )
    else:
        print("\nDA CHON DU 17 DIEM!")
        print("Nhan S de luu toa do.")


# ==========================================
# TAO 64 TOA DO
# ==========================================

def generate_coordinates():

    # Hai diem dau la True va False cua y a cau 1
    q1_true = points[0]
    q1_false = points[1]

    # Diem thu ba la True cua y d cau 1
    q1_last = points[2]

    # Tinh toa do trung binh hang a
    q1_y = (q1_true[1] + q1_false[1]) / 2

    # Khoang cach giua cac hang
    row_step = (q1_last[1] - q1_y) / 3

    if not (10 <= row_step <= 150):
        raise ValueError(
            "Khoang cach hang khong hop le. "
            "Hay kiem tra lai diem cau 1."
        )

    questions = []

    # Cau 1
    anchors = {
        1: {
            "true_x": q1_true[0],
            "false_x": q1_false[0],
            "start_y": q1_y
        }
    }

    # Cau 2 den cau 8
    index = 3

    for q in range(2, 9):

        true_point = points[index]
        false_point = points[index + 1]

        index += 2

        if abs(true_point[1] - false_point[1]) > 12:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Hai diem Dung/Sai cau {q} "
                "khong cung hang. Hay hieu chuan lai."
            )

        anchors[q] = {
            "true_x": true_point[0],
            "false_x": false_point[0],
            "start_y": (
                true_point[1] + false_point[1]
            ) / 2
        }

    # Sinh 8 cau x 4 y x 2 lua chon = 64 o
    for q in range(1, 9):

        anchor = anchors[q]

        question_data = {
            "question": q,
            "rows": {}
        }

        for row_index, row_label in enumerate(ROWS):

            y = (
                anchor["start_y"]
                + row_index * row_step
            )

            question_data["rows"][row_label] = {
                "true": [
                    round(float(anchor["true_x"]), 2),
                    round(float(y), 2)
                ],
                "false": [
                    round(float(anchor["false_x"]), 2),
                    round(float(y), 2)
                ]
            }

        questions.append(question_data)

    return questions, row_step


# ==========================================
# LUU TOA DO VA ANH KIEM TRA
# ==========================================

def save_calibration():

    if len(points) != 17:
        print("Chua chon du 17 diem.")
        return

    questions, row_step = generate_coordinates()

    result = {
        "template": "answer_sheet_v1",
        "template_width": width,
        "template_height": height,
        "row_step": round(row_step, 3),
        "questions": questions
    }

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "part2_coordinates.json"

    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            result,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    # Ve 64 vi tri len anh mau
    preview = image.copy()

    for question in questions:

        q = question["question"]

        for row_label in ROWS:

            row = question["rows"][row_label]

            for choice in ["true", "false"]:

                x, y = row[choice]

                x = int(round(x))
                y = int(round(y))

                cv2.circle(
                    preview,
                    (x, y),
                    9,
                    (0, 180, 0),
                    2
                )

                label = (
                    str(q) + row_label
                    + ("T" if choice == "true" else "S")
                )

                cv2.putText(
                    preview,
                    label,
                    (x + 8, y - 4),
                    cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                    0.25,
                    (0, 0, 255),
                    1
                )

    preview_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR / "part2_calibration_preview.png"
    )

    cv2.imwrite(
        str(preview_path),
        preview
    )

    print("\n================================")
    print("HIEU CHUAN PHAN II THANH CONG!")
    print("So cau:", len(questions))
    print("So o:", len(questions) * 4 * 2)
    print("Khoang cach hang:", round(row_step, 2))
    print("File toa do:", json_path.resolve())
    print("Anh kiem tra:", preview_path.resolve())
    print("================================")


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

print("HUONG DAN HIEU CHUAN PHAN II")
print("--------------------------------")

for i, label in enumerate(CLICK_LABELS, start=1):
    print(f"{i}. {label}")

print("\nClick dung TAM cac o tron.")
print("S: Luu khi da chon du 17 diem.")
print("ESC: Huy.")

cv2.namedWindow(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    cv2.WINDOW_NORMAL
)

cv2.resizeWindow(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    display_width,
    display_height
)

cv2.setMouseCallback(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    mouse_callback
)

while True:

    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)

    key = cv2.waitKey(20) & 0xFF

    if key == 27:
        print("Da huy hieu chuan.")
        break

    if key == ord("s"):

        try:
            save_calibration()
            break

        except Exception as error:
            print("LOI:", error)

cv2.destroyAllWindows()

[36/38] TẬP TIN: tools/calibration/calibrate_part3.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import cv2
import json
from pathlib import Path

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

IMAGE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

QUESTION_COUNT = 6
DIGITS = list(range(10))
DIGIT_COLUMNS = 4

# ==========================================
# TAO DANH SACH 28 DIEM CLICK
# ==========================================

CLICK_LABELS = [
    "Cau 1 - Chu so 0 - Cot 1",
    "Cau 1 - Chu so 0 - Cot 2",
    "Cau 1 - Chu so 0 - Cot 3",
    "Cau 1 - Chu so 0 - Cot 4",

    "Cau 1 - Chu so 9 - Cot 1",

    "Cau 1 - Dau am",
    "Cau 1 - Dau phay vi tri 1",
    "Cau 1 - Dau phay vi tri 2",
]

for q in range(2, 7):
    for col in range(1, 5):
        CLICK_LABELS.append(
            f"Cau {q} - Chu so 0 - Cot {col}"
        )

# ==========================================
# DOC ANH PHIEU MAU
# ==========================================

image = cv2.imread(str(IMAGE_PATH))

if image is None:
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Khong tim thay anh: {IMAGE_PATH}"
    )

height, width = image.shape[:2]

# Thu nho anh vua man hinh
scale = min(
    760 / height,
    1100 / width,
    1.0
)

display_width = int(width * scale)
display_height = int(height * scale)

scale_x = display_width / width
scale_y = display_height / height

display_original = cv2.resize(
    image,
    (display_width, display_height),
    interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA
)

display_image = display_original.copy()

points = []

WINDOW_NAME = "HIEU CHUAN PHAN III"

# ==========================================
# VE CAC DIEM DA CHON
# ==========================================

def redraw():

    global display_image

    display_image = display_original.copy()

    for index, (x, y) in enumerate(points):

        px = int(round(x * scale_x))
        py = int(round(y * scale_y))

        cv2.circle(
            display_image,
            (px, py),
            5,
            (0, 255, 0),
            2
        )

        cv2.putText(
            display_image,
            str(index + 1),
            (px + 5, py - 5),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.4,
            (0, 0, 255),
            1
        )

    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)


# ==========================================
# CLICK CHUOT
# ==========================================

def mouse_callback(event, x, y, flags, param):

    if event != cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        return

    if len(points) >= len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print("Da chon du 28 diem.")
        return

    original_x = int(round(x / scale_x))
    original_y = int(round(y / scale_y))

    points.append((original_x, original_y))

    index = len(points) - 1

    print(
        f"{index + 1}/28 - "
        f"{CLICK_LABELS[index]}: "
        f"x={original_x}, y={original_y}"
    )

    redraw()

    if len(points) < len(CLICK_LABELS):
        print(
            "Tiep theo:",
            CLICK_LABELS[len(points)]
        )
    else:
        print("\nDA CHON DU 28 DIEM!")
        print("Nhan S de luu toa do.")


# ==========================================
# KIEM TRA VA TAO TOA DO
# ==========================================

def save_calibration():

    if len(points) != 28:
        print("Chua chon du 28 diem.")
        return

    # Toa do hang chu so 0 va 9 cua cau 1
    row_zero = points[0][1]
    row_nine = points[4][1]

    row_step = (row_nine - row_zero) / 9

    if not (12 <= row_step <= 80):
        raise ValueError(
            "Khoang cach hang khong hop le."
        )

    # Dau am va dau phay
    sign_y = points[5][1]

    comma_y1 = points[6][1]
    comma_y2 = points[7][1]

    if abs(comma_y1 - comma_y2) > 12:
        raise ValueError(
            "Hai diem dau phay khong cung hang."
        )

    comma_y = (comma_y1 + comma_y2) / 2

    # Lay 4 toa do cot chu so cua 6 cau
    x_groups = []

    # Cau 1
    x_groups.append([
        points[i][0]
        for i in range(4)
    ])

    # Cau 2 den cau 6
    index = 8

    for q in range(2, 7):

        xs = [
            points[index + i][0]
            for i in range(4)
        ]

        index += 4

        x_groups.append(xs)

    # Kiem tra cac cot tang dan
    for q, xs in enumerate(x_groups, start=1):

        if not all(
            xs[i] < xs[i + 1]
            for i in range(3)
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"Toa do cot chu so cau {q} khong hop le."
            )

    # Kiem tra vi tri dau am va dau phay
    ref_xs = x_groups[0]

    if abs(points[5][0] - ref_xs[0]) > 20:
        raise ValueError(
            "Vi tri dau am khong dung cot 1."
        )

    if abs(points[6][0] - ref_xs[1]) > 20:
        raise ValueError(
            "Dau phay vi tri 1 khong dung."
        )

    if abs(points[7][0] - ref_xs[2]) > 20:
        raise ValueError(
            "Dau phay vi tri 2 khong dung."
        )

    # ======================================
    # SINH TOA DO 6 CAU
    # ======================================

    questions = []

    for q in range(QUESTION_COUNT):

        xs = x_groups[q]

        question_data = {
            "question": q + 1,

            "sign": [
                round(float(xs[0]), 2),
                round(float(sign_y), 2)
            ],

            "comma_positions": [
                [
                    round(float(xs[1]), 2),
                    round(float(comma_y), 2)
                ],
                [
                    round(float(xs[2]), 2),
                    round(float(comma_y), 2)
                ]
            ],

            "digits": {}
        }

        for digit in DIGITS:

            y = row_zero + digit * row_step

            question_data["digits"][str(digit)] = [
                [
                    round(float(x), 2),
                    round(float(y), 2)
                ]
                for x in xs
            ]

        questions.append(question_data)

    result = {
        "template": "answer_sheet_v1",
        "template_width": width,
        "template_height": height,
        "row_step": round(row_step, 3),
        "questions": questions
    }

    # ======================================
    # LUU JSON
    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "part3_coordinates.json"

    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            result,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    # ======================================
    # VE ANH KIEM TRA
    # ======================================

    preview = image.copy()

    for question in questions:

        # Dau am - xanh duong
        x, y = question["sign"]

        cv2.circle(
            preview,
            (int(x), int(y)),
            8,
            (255, 0, 0),
            2
        )

        # Dau phay - mau vang
        for x, y in question["comma_positions"]:

            cv2.circle(
                preview,
                (int(x), int(y)),
                8,
                (0, 165, 255),
                2
            )

        # 40 o chu so cua moi cau - xanh la
        for digit, positions in question["digits"].items():

            for x, y in positions:

                cv2.circle(
                    preview,
                    (int(x), int(y)),
                    7,
                    (0, 180, 0),
                    2
                )

    preview_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR / "part3_calibration_preview.png"
    )

    cv2.imwrite(
        str(preview_path),
        preview
    )

    print("\n================================")
    print("HIEU CHUAN PHAN III THANH CONG!")
    print("So cau:", len(questions))
    print("So o chu so:", 240)
    print("So dau am:", 6)
    print("So vi tri dau phay:", 12)
    print("Khoang cach hang:", round(row_step, 2))
    print("File toa do:", json_path.resolve())
    print("Anh kiem tra:", preview_path.resolve())
    print("================================")


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

print("HIEU CHUAN PHAN III")
print("--------------------------------")

for i, label in enumerate(CLICK_LABELS, start=1):
    print(f"{i}. {label}")

print("\nClick vao TAM cac o tron.")
print("S: Luu toa do khi da chon du 28 diem.")
print("Backspace: Xoa diem vua chon.")
print("ESC: Huy.")

cv2.namedWindow(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    cv2.WINDOW_NORMAL
)

cv2.resizeWindow(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    display_width,
    display_height
)

cv2.setMouseCallback(
    WINDOW_NAME,
    mouse_callback
)

while True:

    cv2.imshow(WINDOW_NAME, display_image)

    key = cv2.waitKey(20) & 0xFF

    if key == 27:
        print("Da huy.")
        break

    elif key in (8, 127):

        if points:
            removed = points.pop()
            print("Da xoa diem:", removed)
            redraw()

    elif key == ord("s"):

        try:
            save_calibration()
            break

        except Exception as error:
            print("LOI:", error)

cv2.destroyAllWindows()

[38/38] TẬP TIN: tools/calibration/convert_pdf.py

import fitz
from pathlib import Path

# Tim file PDF mau trong thu muc du an
pdf_files = list(Path(".").glob("*.pdf"))

if not pdf_files:
    print("LOI: Khong tim thay file PDF mau!")
else:
    pdf_path = pdf_files[0]
    output_dir = Path("output")
    output_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    print("Dang doc file:", pdf_path.name)

    # Mo file PDF
    document = fitz.open(pdf_path)

    # Chi xu ly trang dau tien
    page = document[0]

    # Tang do phan giai anh len 3 lan
    matrix = fitz.Matrix(3, 3)

    # Chuyen trang PDF thanh anh
    pixmap = page.get_pixmap(
        matrix=matrix,
        alpha=False
    )

    # Luu anh PNG
    output_path = output_dir / "answer_sheet_master.png"
    pixmap.save(output_path)

    print("--------------------------------")
    print("CHUYEN DOI THANH CONG!")
    print("So trang PDF:", len(document))
    print("Kich thuoc anh:", pixmap.width, "x", pixmap.height)
    print("Anh duoc luu tai:", output_path.resolve())
    print("--------------------------------")

    document.close()
